# Universal Asset Ripper

<p align="center">
  <img src="docs/media/banner.png" alt="Universal Asset Ripper Banner" width="100%">
</p>

<p align="center">
  <strong>High-performance multi-engine game asset extractor, native container parser, and AI-assisted reverse engineering orchestrator.</strong>
</p>

<p align="center">
  <a href="#-features"><img src="https://img.shields.io/badge/status-active-brightgreen.svg" alt="Status"></a>
  <a href="https://nodejs.org/"><img src="https://img.shields.io/badge/node-%3E%3D22.0.0-blue.svg" alt="Node Version"></a>

</p>

## 📖 Overview

**Universal Asset Ripper** is an engine-agnostic extraction and binary reverse engineering platform designed for game developers, modders, and security researchers. It seamlessly bridges native binary format parsers with AI pair-programming agents via the **Model Context Protocol (MCP)** and integrates into an advanced software reverse engineering (SRE) toolchain.

Whether analyzing **Godot** archives, **Unreal Engine** IoStore/PAK containers, **Unity** IL2CPP metadata, or desktop **Windows PE** executables, Universal Asset Ripper provides lightning-fast heuristic detection, categorized asset inventories, targeted extraction, and deep binary entropy inspection for cryptographic keys.

---

## 🎯 Features

* 🔍 **Automatic Engine & Framework Detection (`ripper detect`) :**
  - Heuristics and signature analysis for **Unity**, **Unreal Engine**, and **Godot**.
  - Application framework detection (**Delphi**, **.NET**, **Electron**, **Qt**, **Native C/C++**).
  - PE header architecture analysis (x86, x64, ARM64) and encryption flagging.
* 📦 **100% Native Pure TypeScript Parsers :**
  - **Godot PCK :** Zero-dependency parser for standalone `.pck` packages and embedded executables (`GDPC` magic) supporting format versions 1 through 4.
  - **Windows PE (.rsrc) :** Complete multi-resolution `.ico` icon reconstruction, Delphi `.dfm` form extraction, XML manifests, bitmaps, and raw `RCDATA`.
* 📊 **Asset Manifest & Inspection (`ripper inspect`) :**
  - Full cataloging across unified categories: textures, 3D meshes, audio, scenes, scripts, shaders, fonts, videos, and manifests.
* ⚡ **Targeted Extraction (`ripper extract`) :**
  - Category filtering (`--categories texture,audio,mesh`) and regex/glob filename filtering (`--filter`).
  - Native support for **AES-256** decryption keys (`--aes-key <hex>`) for Unreal Engine packages.
* 🤖 **AI-Assisted Binary Analysis (`ripper rea-inspect`) :**
  - Sliding-window Shannon entropy scanning to spot 256-bit encryption keys.
  - Proximity search around Rijndael S-Box lookup tables (`0x63, 0x7c, 0x77, 0x7b...`).
  - Header sniffing and version validation for Unity IL2CPP `global-metadata.dat`.
  - Automated generation of structured **Evidence Bundles** formatted for AI agents.
* 🔌 **Integrated Model Context Protocol (MCP) Server (`ripper mcp`) :**
  - Exposes extraction, inspection, and scanning tools over standard I/O for Claude Code, Gemini CLI, Cursor, and Antigravity.
* 🎨 **Downstream Modding Pipeline Ready :**
  - Feeds extracted assets and decrypted structures directly into generative game modding frameworks such as [rehan-remade/universal-modder](https://github.com/rehan-remade/universal-modder) (fal.ai / ComfyUI asset replacement and in-game testing).

---

## 🏗️ Architecture

```
                                ┌────────────────────────────────┐
                                │   Universal Asset Ripper       │
                                │   (CLI & MCP stdio Server)     │
                                └──────────────┬─────────────────┘
                                               │
                    ┌──────────────────────────┴──────────────────────────┐
                    ▼                                                     ▼
      [ EngineDetector Engine ]                                 [ ReaBridge & AI Hub ]
  (Unity, Unreal, Godot, Windows PE)                        (BinaryScanner, Shannon Entropy,
                    │                                         AES-256 discovery, Evidence)
       ┌────────────┼────────────┬────────────┐                           │
       ▼            ▼            ▼            ▼              ┌────────────┼────────────┐
  [ Godot ]    [ Unreal ]    [ Unity ]   [ PE Soft. ]        ▼            ▼            ▼
  Adapter       Adapter       Adapter     Adapter       [ Ghidra ]   [ Cpp2IL ]    [ Frida ]
  (Native       (repak /    (AssetRipper (Resource      (Native SRE   (IL2CPP ->   (Runtime
  PCK reader)  CUE4Parse)    CLI bridge)  Extractor)    Decompiler)    C# .NET)     Hooking)
```

---

## 🚀 Installation & Quick Start

### Prerequisites
* **Node.js** >= 22.0.0
* **npm** >= 10.0.0

### Global CLI Installation
Clone and build the repository locally:

```bash
git clone https://github.com/laurentvv/universal-asset-ripper.git
cd universal-asset-ripper
npm install
npm run build
npm link
```

Verify your installation:
```bash
ripper --help
```

---

## 💻 CLI Usage

### 1. Detect Game Engine & Framework
Inspect any folder, game directory, or executable:
```bash
ripper detect "C:/Games/MyGame"
# Or point directly to an executable:
ripper detect "C:/Games/MyGame/MyGame.exe"
```

### 2. Inspect Package Manifest
Display categorized file counts and size breakdowns without writing to disk:
```bash
ripper inspect "C:/Games/MyGame/game.pck"
```

### 3. Extract Assets
Extract all assets or filter by specific categories:
```bash
# Full extraction
ripper extract "C:/Games/MyGame" -o "./extracted"

# Extract only textures and audio files
ripper extract "C:/Games/MyGame" -o "./extracted" --categories texture,audio

# Extract Unreal PAK with AES key
ripper extract "C:/Games/MyGame/Content/Paks/PakChunk0.pak" -o "./extracted" --aes-key 0x4A6B...
```

### 4. Deep Binary Inspection (AI Evidence Bundle)
Scan an executable for cryptographic keys, S-Box constants, and IL2CPP metadata:
```bash
ripper rea-inspect "C:/Games/MyGame/Game-Win64-Shipping.exe"
```

---

## 🤖 MCP Server Setup (AI Agents)

Universal Asset Ripper runs as a standard **Model Context Protocol (MCP)** server over `stdio`. It connects natively with **Claude Code**, **Gemini CLI**, **Cursor**, and **Antigravity**.

Add the server to your `mcpServers` configuration file:

```json
{
  "mcpServers": {
    "universal-asset-ripper": {
      "command": "node",
      "args": ["C:/git/universal-asset-ripper/dist/cli/index.js", "mcp"]
    }
  }
}
```

### Tools Exposed to Agents
| Tool Name | Description |
| :--- | :--- |
| `ripper_detect` | Run heuristic analysis to detect the game engine, architecture, and container type. |
| `ripper_inspect` | Return a complete manifest of files with counts and sizes grouped by category. |
| `ripper_extract` | Extract assets to disk with category filters and optional AES-256 decryption keys. |
| `ripper_scan_binary` | Run Shannon entropy scans, find AES keys, and extract IL2CPP metadata headers. |

---

## 🔬 Reverse Engineering Ecosystem

Universal Asset Ripper forms the upstream reconnaissance and extraction hub within a comprehensive SRE toolchain:

| Tool | Category | Specialty & Role in Pipeline |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **[morluto/rea](https://github.com/morluto/rea)** | Agentic SRE | Autonomous LLM agents consuming Evidence Bundles to plan reverse engineering steps. |
| **[Ghidra](https://github.com/NationalSecurityAgency/ghidra)** | Static SRE | Deep native disassembly and C pseudocode decompilation (x86/x64/ARM). |
| **[Cpp2IL](https://github.com/SamboyCoding/Cpp2IL)** | Unity IL2CPP | Reconstructs executable CIL (.NET) bytecode from `GameAssembly.dll` for decompilation in dnSpy/ILSpy. |
| **[JADX](https://github.com/skylot/jadx)** | Android SRE | Dex-to-Java decompiler for unpacking APK/AAB packages, manifests, and JNI bridges. |
| **[Frida](https://github.com/frida/frida)** | Dynamic SRE | Runtime memory hooking to capture live AES keys and dump uncompressed asset buffers. |
| **[universal-modder](https://github.com/rehan-remade/universal-modder)** | Modding Studio | Downstream mod injection, generative AI assets (fal.ai/ComfyUI), and in-game automated testing. |

The test suite validates parser accuracy against synthetically generated binary containers (PE headers, Godot PCK archives, and AES memory blocks):

```bash
npm test
```

`docs/architecture.md`:

```md
# Universal Asset Ripper - Architecture Documentation

## 1. Overview

**Universal Asset Ripper** is a modular, multi-engine extraction and reverse engineering orchestrator for video games developed with **Unity**, **Unreal Engine**, and **Godot**. It couples native container parsers with engine-specialized tools and the **REA (`morluto/rea`)** AI agent framework.

It operates in three modes:
1. **Interactive CLI (`ripper`)**: For quick detection, inspection, and extraction from terminals.
2. **MCP Server (`ripper mcp`)**: Standard Model Context Protocol server exposing tools over `stdio` to AI pair-programming agents (Gemini, Claude, Cursor, Antigravity).
3. **TypeScript/Node.js Library**: Direct programmatic API via `import { EngineDetector, AdapterRegistry, ReaBridge } from "universal-asset-ripper"`.

---

## 2. Architectural Blueprint

```
                      ┌────────────────────────────────────────┐
                      │        Universal Asset Ripper          │
                      │  (CLI: commander / MCP: stdio server)  │
                      └───────────────────┬────────────────────┘
                                          │
            ┌─────────────────────────────┴─────────────────────────────┐
            ▼                                                           ▼
┌──────────────────────────────┐                           ┌───────────────────────────┐
│     EngineDetector           │                           │        ReaBridge          │
│ (Signatures, PE Arch, Magic) │                           │  (BinaryScanner, Entropy, │
└──────────────┬───────────────┘                           │   AES search, IL2CPP)     │
               │                                           └───────────────────────────┘
               ▼
┌──────────────────────────────┐
│       AdapterRegistry        │
└──────────────┬───────────────┘
               │
   ┌───────────┼───────────┬───────────┐
   ▼           ▼           ▼           ▼
┌──────────┐┌──────────┐┌──────────┐┌──────────┐
│  Godot   ││  Unreal  ││  Unity   ││ PE Soft. │
│ Adapter  ││ Adapter  ││ Adapter  ││ Adapter  │
└────┬─────┘└────┬─────┘└────┬─────┘└────┬─────┘
     │           │           │           │
     ▼           ▼           ▼           ▼
[Native PCK] [repak/   [AssetRipper/  [PE .rsrc /
 [Parser]     CUE4Parse] UnityFS]      Icons/DFM]
```

---

## 3. Core Components

### 3.1. `EngineDetector` (`src/core/detector/engine-detector.ts`)
Heuristic analysis engine that inspects directory trees and binary file headers:
* **Unity:** Detects `UnityPlayer.dll`, `*_Data` directories, `globalgamemanagers`, `data.unity3d`, and discriminates between **Mono** (`MonoBleedingEdge`, `Managed/`) and **IL2CPP** (`GameAssembly.dll`, `il2cpp_data`).
* **Unreal Engine:** Detects `Engine/Binaries`, `Content/Paks`, `.pak` (reads `FPakInfo`), and Zen Store IoStore containers (`.utoc`, `.ucas`).
* **Godot:** Detects standalone `.pck` files and self-contained executables containing embedded PCK archives (`GDPC` magic). Sniffs exact engine version (`major.minor.patch`).

### 3.2. Native Binary Parsers (`src/parsers/`)
* **Godot PCK Parser (`src/parsers/godot/pck-parser.ts`)**:
  - Pure TypeScript binary buffer reader.
  - Supports PCK format versions 1, 2, 3, and 4.
  - Automatically identifies whether the PCK is standalone or appended to a Windows PE `.exe`.
  - Parses file count, path lengths, `res://` paths, offsets, file sizes, MD5 hashes, and encryption flags.
  - Extracts assets directly to disk without requiring external tooling.
* **Unreal PAK Parser (`src/parsers/unreal/pak-parser.ts`)**:
  - Reads `FPakInfo` located in the footer of `.pak` archives (magic `0x5A6F12E1`).
  - Reads version number, index offset, index size, and AES encryption flag.
* **PE Resource Parser (`src/parsers/pe/pe-resource-parser.ts`)**:
  - Traverses Windows PE directory trees across 3 levels (Types, Names/IDs, Languages).
  - Reconstructs complete `.ico` files from `RT_GROUP_ICON` and `RT_ICON`.
  - Extracts `RT_MANIFEST` as XML, `RT_BITMAP` as BMP, and `RT_RCDATA` (Delphi DFM forms, certificates, locales).
  - Sniffs application framework (.NET, Delphi, Electron, Qt, Native C/C++).
* **Unity Bundle Parser (`src/parsers/unity/bundle-parser.ts`)**:
  - Identifies `UnityFS`, `UnityWeb`, `UnityRaw`, and `SerializedFile` headers.
  - Extracts engine release version strings (e.g., `2021.3.16f1`).

### 3.3. Adapters (`src/adapters/`)
All engine and software adapters inherit from `BaseEngineAdapter` and implement `IEngineAdapter`:
* `GodotAdapter` (native PCK parsing & extraction)
* `UnrealAdapter` (Unreal PAK & IoStore, AES decryption support)
* `UnityAdapter` (Unity assets, scenes, AssetRipper CLI bridge)
* `PeSoftwareAdapter` (Windows PE desktop software, icons, DFM, manifests, resources)
* `canHandle(targetPath: string): Promise<boolean>`
* `inspect(targetPath: string): Promise<AssetManifest>`
* `extract(targetPath: string, options: ExtractOptions): Promise<ExtractionResult>`

Adapters aggregate asset items into unified categories:
* `texture`, `mesh`, `audio`, `scene`, `script`, `shader`, `font`, `video`, `animation`, `config`, `other`.

### 3.4. REA Bridge & Binary Scanner (`src/rea/`)
* **`BinaryScanner`**:
  - Calculates Shannon entropy across byte windows.
  - Discovers 256-bit candidate AES encryption keys in hex strings or binary arrays near Rijndael S-Box lookup tables (`0x63, 0x7c, 0x77, 0x7b...`).
  - Validates IL2CPP `global-metadata.dat` magic (`0xFAB11BAF`) and extracts metadata version.
* **`ReaBridge`**:
  - Generates structured *Evidence Bundles* conforming to the `morluto/rea` agent protocol.
  - Interfaces with the `rea` CLI and AI agent decompiler workflows (Ghidra, Cpp2IL, JADX, Frida, Hopper, IDA).

### 3.5. External Reverse Engineering Toolchain Ecosystem
When automated heuristics and native parsers encounter locked or obfuscated containers:
* **`morluto/rea`**: High-level reasoning and agentic workflow orchestration.
* **`Ghidra`**: Static binary decompilation of shipping native executables and libraries.
* **`Cpp2IL`**: Ahead-of-Time Unity IL2CPP deobfuscation and CIL bytecode reconstruction.
* **`JADX`**: Dalvik/ART Dex-to-Java decompilation for Android packages (`.apk`).
* **`Frida`**: Dynamic instrumentation and memory interception of runtime keys and buffers.

### 3.6. Model Context Protocol (MCP) Server (`src/mcp/server.ts`)
Exposes four tools over JSON-RPC stdio:
1. `ripper_detect`: Returns engine type, confidence, version, architecture, and signature markers.
2. `ripper_inspect`: Returns complete file count and size breakdowns by category.
3. `ripper_extract`: Performs asset extraction with category filtering and AES key injection.
4. `ripper_scan_binary`: Runs deep binary analysis for candidate AES keys and IL2CPP metadata.

### 3.7. Downstream Ecosystem: Creative Game Modding (`universal-modder`)
Extracted assets and decrypted structures directly empower downstream modding agent suites like **[rehan-remade/universal-modder](https://github.com/rehan-remade/universal-modder)**. While Universal Asset Ripper handles the upstream reconnaissance and extraction layer, `universal-modder` consumes these assets to drive AI asset generation (fal.ai / ComfyUI), code injection, and automated in-game verification.

```

`docs/engines.md`:

```md
# Engine Deep Dive & Tooling Guide

This document describes internal container structures, extraction workflows, and recommended toolchains for each supported engine.

---

## 1. Unity

### Container Formats
* **AssetBundles (`.bundle`, `.unity3d`)**: Compressed container archive starting with `UnityFS` (LZ4 / LZMA compression) or legacy `UnityWeb` / `UnityRaw`.
* **Serialized Files (`.assets`, `.sharedassets`)**: Binary serialized scene and object trees containing TypeTrees or serialized type definitions.
* **Global Game Managers (`globalgamemanagers`)**: Root project settings and build configurations.

### Runtime Architectures
* **Mono (.NET CIL)**:
  - Bytecode stored in `.dll` files inside `<Game>_Data/Managed/`.
  - Decompilable using standard .NET decompiler tools (dnSpy, ILSpy, dotPeek).
* **IL2CPP (Ahead-of-Time Native)**:
  - C# code compiled directly to native machine code (`GameAssembly.dll`).
  - Class definitions, field names, and method metadata stored in `<Game>_Data/il2cpp_data/Metadata/global-metadata.dat`.
  - Can be inspected using `ripper rea-inspect` or restored using `Il2CppDumper`.

### Recommended External Tooling
* [AssetRipper](https://github.com/AssetRipper/AssetRipper):
  - CLI installation: `dotnet tool install -g AssetRipper.Cli`
  - Fully converts Unity assets into standard glTF, FBX, WAV, PNG, and rebuilds complete Unity project hierarchies.
* [Cpp2IL](https://github.com/SamboyCoding/Cpp2IL):
  - In-depth reverse engineering tool for Unity IL2CPP builds.
  - Unlike `Il2CppDumper` (which only recovers signatures and empty dummy DLLs with `throw null;`), **Cpp2IL** analyzes machine code in `GameAssembly.dll` and reconstructs executable **CIL bytecode** within method bodies.
  - Produces valid .NET assemblies that can be decompiled into readable C# using [dnSpy](https://github.com/dnSpy/dnSpy) or [ILSpy](https://github.com/icsharpcode/ILSpy).
* [Il2CppDumper](https://github.com/Perfare/Il2CppDumper):
  - Fast extraction of type declarations, string literals, method addresses, and symbol scripts for Ghidra / IDA.

---

## 2. Unreal Engine

### Container Formats
* **Legacy PAK (`.pak`)**:
  - Container format used from UE4.0 through UE5+.
  - Index and file list stored at an offset pointed to by the footer struct `FPakInfo` (magic `0x5A6F12E1`).
  - Index or file payload can be encrypted with **AES-256**.
* **Zen Store IoStore (`.utoc`, `.ucas`)**:
  - Introduced in UE4.26, standard in UE5.
  - `.utoc` (Table of Contents): Container metadata, chunk hashes, and compression info.
  - `.ucas` (Container Archive Storage): Raw file chunks.

### AES-256 Encryption & Keys
When packages are encrypted, an AES-256 key is required.
* Keys can often be found in the shipping executable or DLL using:
  ```bash
  ripper rea-inspect <Game>-Win64-Shipping.exe
  ```
* Once identified, extract with:
  ```bash
  ripper extract Content/Paks/PakChunk0.pak -o extracted/ --aes-key 0xYOUR_HEX_KEY
  ```

### Advanced Key Recovery (Static & Dynamic)
* **Static Analysis with [Ghidra](https://github.com/NationalSecurityAgency/ghidra)**:
  - If the key is obfuscated or compiled via custom templates, load `<Game>-Win64-Shipping.exe` in Ghidra.
  - Search for references to strings like `AES_set_decrypt_key`, `FPakPlatformFile`, or `FEncryptionKeyRegistration`.
* **Dynamic Hooking with [Frida](https://github.com/frida/frida)**:
  - For games with heavy anti-reverse or dynamic key derivation, attach Frida to the running game process to intercept the decryption key directly from memory as it is passed to the decryption routine:
    ```javascript
    // Example: hook AES key parameter in runtime memory
    const decryptFunc = Module.findExportByName(null, "AES_set_decrypt_key");
    if (decryptFunc) {
      Interceptor.attach(decryptFunc, {
        onEnter(args) {
          console.log("[Frida] AES Key buffer:", hexdump(args[0], { length: 32 }));
        }
      });
    }
    ```

### Recommended External Tooling
* [repak](https://github.com/trumank/repak):
  - High-performance Rust CLI for unpacking and packing `.pak` files.
  - Installation: `cargo install repak`
* [CUE4Parse](https://github.com/FabianFG/CUE4Parse) / [FModel](https://fmodel.app):
  - Industry standard C# library for reading `.pak`, `.utoc`, `.ucas`, `.usmap` mapping tables, and deserializing `.uasset` files into 3D models and textures.

---

## 3. Godot Engine

### Container Formats
* **PCK (`.pck`)**:
  - Standard package archive.
  - Header starts with magic `GDPC` (`0x43504447`).
  - Contains format version (1 to 4) and engine version (`major.minor.patch`).
  - Indexed table maps `res://` paths to offsets and lengths.
* **Embedded PCK in Executable (`.exe`)**:
  - Standalone release binaries for Windows and Linux bundle the `.pck` package directly into the executable file.
  - A 12-byte footer (`[int64 offset] [magic GDPC]`) at the end of the binary references the embedded PCK.

### Native Extraction
**Universal Asset Ripper** includes a 100% native TypeScript binary parser for Godot PCK packages. No external tool is required to extract assets:
```bash
ripper extract Game.pck -o extracted/
# Or directly from the executable:
ripper extract Game.exe -o extracted/
```

### Script & Scene Restoration
* [GDRE Tools](https://github.com/bruvzg/gdsdecomp):
  - Headless Godot fork capable of decompiling compiled GDScript bytecode (`.gdc`) and restoring `.tscn` text scenes.

---

## 4. Mobile & Android Containers (APK / AAB / XAPK)

### Structure & Ripping Challenges
* Android games package standard engine assets into ZIP containers:
  - Bytecode: `classes.dex` (Dalvik / ART)
  - Native Engine Binaries: `lib/<arch>/` (`libunity.so`, `libil2cpp.so`, `libUE4.so`, `libgodot_android.so`)
  - Assets & Containers: `assets/`, `res/`, or external expansion files (`.obb`)
* Many games protect asset decryption keys in Java wrapper code, or through JNI bridges connecting to native libraries.

### Recommended Tooling
* [JADX](https://github.com/skylot/jadx):
  - High quality Dex-to-Java decompiler with GUI and CLI (`jadx -d out/ app.apk`).
  - Decodes `AndroidManifest.xml`, resource tables, and Dalvik bytecode back to readable Java/Kotlin code.
  - Indispensable for identifying how assets are decrypted before being fed into native engine runtimes.
* [Frida](https://github.com/frida/frida):
  - Dynamic instrumentation on Android devices / emulators (`frida-server` or `frida-gadget`).
  - Allows hooking JNI functions, bypassing SSL pinning, and dumping decrypted asset buffers directly from memory.
* [Cpp2IL](https://github.com/SamboyCoding/Cpp2IL) + [Ghidra](https://github.com/NationalSecurityAgency/ghidra):
  - For Unity IL2CPP games on Android, run Cpp2IL against `lib/arm64-v8a/libil2cpp.so` + `assets/bin/Data/Managed/Metadata/global-metadata.dat` to extract full C# game code.

```

`docs/rea-guide.md`:

```md
# REA (Reverse Engineer Anything) Integration Guide

## 1. Context & Motivation

When extracting game assets, standard tools often encounter barriers:
1. **Unreal Engine:** `.pak` or `.ucas` packages encrypted with AES-256 where the key is unknown.
2. **Unity:** IL2CPP builds where C# bytecode has been converted to machine code, or obfuscated `global-metadata.dat`.
3. **Custom Packers:** Proprietary encryption wrappers around standard game engines.

To resolve these challenges without manual static disassembly, **Universal Asset Ripper** connects to **[morluto/rea](https://github.com/morluto/rea)** (`rea-agents`).

---

## 2. Automated Deep Binary Inspection

Run the built-in scanner to identify candidate keys and architectural properties:

```bash
ripper rea-inspect path/to/Game-Win64-Shipping.exe
```

### What `BinaryScanner` Analyzes:
1. **Shannon Entropy Analysis:** Identifies 32-byte (256-bit) blocks of high randomness characteristic of cryptographic keys.
2. **Hex Pattern Matching:** Detects strings matching 64-character hexadecimal patterns (`0x[A-Fa-f0-9]{64}`).
3. **Cryptographic Proximity:** Scans for Rijndael AES SubBytes S-Box constants (`0x63, 0x7c, 0x77, 0x7b...`) and analyzes adjacent memory blocks.
4. **IL2CPP Metadata Headers:** Inspects `global-metadata.dat` for the magic signature `0xFAB11BAF` and metadata version.

---

## 3. Evidence Bundle Format

When `ReaBridge.inspectBinary()` runs, it outputs an **Evidence Bundle** formatted for AI agents:

```json
{
  "targetBinary": "C:/Games/Title/Game-Win64-Shipping.exe",
  "architecture": "x64",
  "evidenceType": "GameBinaryAnalysis",
  "timestamp": "2026-10-11T13:00:00.000Z",
  "findings": {
    "candidateAesKeys": [
      {
        "key": "0x4a6b8d1f3c5e7a9b0c2d4e6f8a1b3c5d7e9f0a2b4c6d8e0f2a4b6c8d0e2f4a6b",
        "offset": 512,
        "confidence": "high"
      }
    ],
    "il2cppVersion": null,
    "signatures": ["UnrealEngine signature", "FArchive"]
  },
  "reaTaskPrompt": "Target: Game-Win64-Shipping.exe (x64)\nCandidate AES keys detected: 0x4a6b...\nObjective: Confirm decryption keys / recovery offsets."
}
```

---

## 4. Using with AI Agents & MCP

Configure `universal-asset-ripper` in your MCP client (e.g. Claude Code, Gemini CLI, Cursor, Antigravity) by adding to your `mcpServers` configuration:

```json
{
  "mcpServers": {
    "universal-asset-ripper": {
      "command": "node",
      "args": ["C:/git/universal-asset-ripper/dist/cli/index.js", "mcp"]
    }
  }
}
```

The AI agent can then directly call:
* `ripper_detect`
* `ripper_inspect`
* `ripper_extract`
* `ripper_scan_binary`

---

## 5. The Multi-Tool Reverse Engineering Ecosystem

REA agents do not reinvent static decompilers or dynamic hooks from scratch; rather, they serve as the **reasoning and orchestration engine** coordinating specialized reverse engineering frameworks:

```
                          ┌─────────────────────────────┐
                          │         morluto/rea         │
                          │ (LLM Orchestration & Plans) │
                          └──────────────┬──────────────┘
                                         │
        ┌───────────────────┬────────────┴───────┬───────────────────┐
        ▼                   ▼                    ▼                   ▼
   [ Ghidra ]           [ Cpp2IL ]            [ JADX ]            [ Frida ]
 Native Decompiler    Unity IL2CPP ->      Android DEX ->     Dynamic Runtime
 (x86/x64/ARM C/ASM)  CIL (.NET DLLs)      Java Decompiler     Hooking & Memory
```

### Role & Division of Responsibilities

1. **[morluto/rea](https://github.com/morluto/rea)**:
   * **Role:** High-level autonomous agent coordinating tasks, generating hypotheses, interpreting Evidence Bundles, and running tools.
   * **When to use:** Complex reverse engineering workflows requiring iterative exploration and decision-making.

2. **[Ghidra](https://github.com/NationalSecurityAgency/ghidra)**:
   * **Role:** Deep static SRE framework for native compiled machine code.
   * **When to use:** Tracing `FAES::Decrypt` in Unreal binaries, auditing Delphi / C++ desktop binaries, reversing custom packing wrappers.

3. **[Cpp2IL](https://github.com/SamboyCoding/Cpp2IL)**:
   * **Role:** Specialized Unity IL2CPP deobfuscator and bytecode reconstructor.
   * **When to use:** When `ripper rea-inspect` detects `global-metadata.dat`. Transforms `GameAssembly.dll` machine code back into real .NET assemblies readable in dnSpy/ILSpy.

4. **[JADX](https://github.com/skylot/jadx)**:
   * **Role:** Dalvik/ART Dex-to-Java decompiler for Android packages (`.apk`, `.dex`, `.aab`).
   * **When to use:** Auditing Android game bootstrap loaders, reading `AndroidManifest.xml`, tracing JNI native library bindings.

5. **[Frida](https://github.com/frida/frida)**:
   * **Role:** Dynamic memory instrumentation and runtime function hooking.
   * **When to use:** When static entropy analysis fails to find AES keys (e.g., dynamically computed or encrypted keys). Intercepts keys and decrypted buffers directly from running processes.

---

## 6. Downstream Integration: Modding & Creative Workflows with `universal-modder`

Once assets are extracted and encryption/code is unraveled, the natural next step is often **modding**, asset replacement, or content creation. 

Universal Asset Ripper feeds directly into **[rehan-remade/universal-modder](https://github.com/rehan-remade/universal-modder)**:

```
      [ UPSTREAM : EXTRACTION & REVERSE ]
      Universal Asset Ripper (MCP Server / CLI)
      + Ghidra / Cpp2IL / Frida / JADX
      Tools: ripper_detect, ripper_inspect, ripper_extract, ripper_scan_binary
                           │
                           │ Extracted Assets & Decompiled Logic
                           ▼
      [ DOWNSTREAM : MOD CREATION & TESTING ]
      universal-modder (Agent Skills: Claude Code, Cursor, Codex)
      + fal.ai / ComfyUI MCP (generative 2D/3D/audio)
      Actions: generate replacement assets, build mod DLL/hooks,
               automate in-game verification, capture showcase video
```

* **MCP Synergy:** AI agents configured with both Universal Asset Ripper's MCP tools and `universal-modder` skills can run complete end-to-end game modding loops automatically: from package sniffing and AES decryption to asset regeneration and live in-game testing.

#[cfg(windows)]
fn main() {
    let mut res = winres::WindowsResource::new();
    res.set_icon("assets/icons/icon_MAINICON.ico");
    // Ignore compile error if rc.exe is not in PATH
    let _ = res.compile();
}

#[cfg(not(windows))]
fn main() {}

`examples/treesize-clone/src/main.rs`:

```rs
use std::fs;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::time::Instant;
use colored::*;
use rayon::prelude::*;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
struct EntryNode {
    path: PathBuf,
    name: String,
    size: u64,
    file_count: u64,
    dir_count: u64,
    children: Vec<EntryNode>,
}

impl EntryNode {
    fn scan(path: &Path) -> Self {
        let name = path
            .file_name()
            .and_then(|n| n.to_str())
            .unwrap_or(path.to_str().unwrap_or("?"))
            .to_string();

        let entries: Vec<PathBuf> = match fs::read_dir(path) {
            Ok(read_dir) => read_dir.filter_map(|e| e.ok().map(|e| e.path())).collect(),
            Err(_) => return Self {
                path: path.to_path_buf(),
                name,
                size: 0,
                file_count: 0,
                dir_count: 0,
                children: Vec::new(),
            },
        };

        let (dirs, files): (Vec<PathBuf>, Vec<PathBuf>) = entries
            .into_iter()
            .partition(|p| p.is_dir());

        // Process direct files
        let mut file_size = 0u64;
        let mut file_count = 0u64;
        for f in &files {
            if let Ok(meta) = fs::symlink_metadata(f) {
                file_size += meta.len();
                file_count += 1;
            }
        }

        // Process subdirectories in parallel with Rayon
        let mut children: Vec<EntryNode> = dirs
            .par_iter()
            .map(|dir| EntryNode::scan(dir))
            .collect();

        // Sort children by size descending (like TreeSize)
        children.sort_by(|a, b| b.size.cmp(&a.size));

        let mut total_size = file_size;
        let mut total_files = file_count;
        let mut total_dirs = children.len() as u64;

        for child in &children {
            total_size += child.size;
            total_files += child.file_count;
            total_dirs += child.dir_count;
        }

        Self {
            path: path.to_path_buf(),
            name,
            size: total_size,
            file_count: total_files,
            dir_count: total_dirs,
            children,
        }
    }

    fn print_tree(&self, prefix: &str, is_last: bool, parent_size: u64, max_depth: usize, current_depth: usize) {
        if current_depth > max_depth {
            return;
        }

        let percent = if parent_size > 0 {
            (self.size as f64 / parent_size as f64) * 100.0
        } else {
            100.0
        };

        let bar_width = 15;
        let filled = ((percent / 100.0) * bar_width as f64).round() as usize;
        let filled = filled.min(bar_width);
        let empty = bar_width.saturating_sub(filled);

        let bar = format!(
            "[{}{}]",
            "█".repeat(filled).yellow(),
            "░".repeat(empty).dimmed()
        );

        let size_formatted = format_bytes(self.size);
        let percent_str = format!("{:>5.1}%", percent).cyan();

        let branch = if current_depth == 0 {
            ""
        } else if is_last {
            "└── "
        } else {
            "├── "
        };

        println!(
            "{}{}{} {:>10}  {} {:>6}  ({:>4} files, {:>3} dirs)",
            prefix.dimmed(),
            branch.dimmed(),
            self.name.bold().green(),
            size_formatted.bold().white(),
            bar,
            percent_str,
            self.file_count,
            self.dir_count
        );

        let next_prefix = if current_depth == 0 {
            "".to_string()
        } else if is_last {
            format!("{}    ", prefix)
        } else {
            format!("{}│   ", prefix)
        };

        for (idx, child) in self.children.iter().enumerate() {
            let last_child = idx == self.children.len() - 1;
            child.print_tree(&next_prefix, last_child, self.size, max_depth, current_depth + 1);
        }
    }
}

fn format_bytes(bytes: u64) -> String {
    const KB: u64 = 1024;
    const MB: u64 = 1024 * KB;
    const GB: u64 = 1024 * MB;
    const TB: u64 = 1024 * GB;

    if bytes >= TB {
        format!("{:.2} TB", bytes as f64 / TB as f64)
    } else if bytes >= GB {
        format!("{:.2} GB", bytes as f64 / GB as f64)
    } else if bytes >= MB {
        format!("{:.2} MB", bytes as f64 / MB as f64)
    } else if bytes >= KB {
        format!("{:.2} KB", bytes as f64 / KB as f64)
    } else {
        format!("{} B", bytes)
    }
}

fn main() {
    let args: Vec<String> = std::env::args().collect();
    let mut target_dir = PathBuf::from(".");
    let mut max_depth = 3usize;

    let mut i = 1;
    while i < args.len() {
        match args[i].as_str() {
            "-d" | "--depth" => {
                if i + 1 < args.len() {
                    max_depth = args[i + 1].parse().unwrap_or(3);
                    i += 1;
                }
            }
            "-h" | "--help" => {
                println!("{}", "TreeSize Clone in Rust (using extracted assets)".bold().cyan());
                println!("Usage: treesize-clone [PATH] [--depth <N>]");
                println!();
                println!("Options:");
                println!("  -d, --depth <N>   Maximum display depth (default: 3)");
                println!("  -h, --help        Show this help message");
                return;
            }
            other => {
                if !other.starts_with('-') {
                    target_dir = PathBuf::from(other);
                }
            }
        }
        i += 1;
    }

    let abs_target = match fs::canonicalize(&target_dir) {
        Ok(p) => p,
        Err(_) => target_dir.clone(),
    };

    println!();
    println!("{}", "═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════".cyan());
    println!("  {} - {}", "TreeSize Rust Clone".bold().yellow(), "High-Performance Disk Scanner".white());
    println!("  Target: {}", abs_target.display().to_string().cyan());
    println!("{}", "═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════".cyan());
    println!();

    let start = Instant::now();
    let root = EntryNode::scan(&abs_target);
    let duration = start.elapsed();

    root.print_tree("", true, root.size, max_depth, 0);

    println!();
    println!("{}", "───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────".dimmed());
    println!(
        "  Scanned: {} in {} files across {} directories",
        format_bytes(root.size).bold().white(),
        root.file_count.to_string().cyan(),
        root.dir_count.to_string().cyan()
    );
    println!("  Time elapsed: {}", format!("{:.2?}", duration).bold().green());
    println!("{}", "───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────".dimmed());
    println!();
}

```

  "devDependencies": {
    "@types/node": "^22.13.9",
    "rimraf": "^6.0.1",
    "typescript": "^5.8.2",
    "vitest": "^3.0.8"
  },
  "engines": {
    "node": ">=22.0.0"
  }
}

`src/adapters/engine-adapter.ts`:

```ts
import * as path from "node:path";
import type {
  AssetCategory,
  AssetItem,
  AssetManifest,
  CategorySummary,
  EngineType,
  ExtractOptions,
  ExtractionResult,
  IEngineAdapter
} from "../types/index.js";

export abstract class BaseEngineAdapter implements IEngineAdapter {
  abstract readonly engine: EngineType;

  abstract canHandle(targetPath: string): Promise<boolean>;
  abstract inspect(targetPath: string): Promise<AssetManifest>;
  abstract extract(targetPath: string, options: ExtractOptions): Promise<ExtractionResult>;

  /**
   * Helper to build a standard AssetManifest from a list of AssetItem
   */
  protected createManifest(
    targetPath: string,
    assets: AssetItem[],
    engineVersion?: string
  ): AssetManifest {
    const byCategory: Record<AssetCategory, CategorySummary> = {
      texture: { count: 0, totalBytes: 0 },
      mesh: { count: 0, totalBytes: 0 },
      audio: { count: 0, totalBytes: 0 },
      scene: { count: 0, totalBytes: 0 },
      script: { count: 0, totalBytes: 0 },
      shader: { count: 0, totalBytes: 0 },
      font: { count: 0, totalBytes: 0 },
      video: { count: 0, totalBytes: 0 },
      animation: { count: 0, totalBytes: 0 },
      config: { count: 0, totalBytes: 0 },
      other: { count: 0, totalBytes: 0 }
    };

    let totalBytes = 0;

    for (const asset of assets) {
      const cat = asset.category;
      if (!byCategory[cat]) {
        byCategory[cat] = { count: 0, totalBytes: 0 };
      }
      byCategory[cat].count++;
      byCategory[cat].totalBytes += asset.size;
      totalBytes += asset.size;
    }

    return {
      engine: this.engine,
      engineVersion,
      targetPath,
      totalFiles: assets.length,
      totalBytes,
      byCategory,
      assets
    };
  }

  /**
   * Filter assets based on ExtractOptions (categories, wildcard / regex filter)
   */
  protected filterAssets(assets: AssetItem[], options: ExtractOptions): AssetItem[] {
    let result = assets;

    if (options.categories && options.categories.length > 0) {
      const allowed = new Set(options.categories);
      result = result.filter((a) => allowed.has(a.category));
    }

    if (options.filter) {
      const filterLower = options.filter.toLowerCase();
      result = result.filter((a) => a.path.toLowerCase().includes(filterLower));
    }

    return result;
  }
}

```

`src/adapters/godot-adapter.ts`:

```ts
import * as fs from "node:fs/promises";
import * as path from "node:path";
import { BaseEngineAdapter } from "./engine-adapter.js";
import { GodotPckParser, type PckFileEntry } from "../parsers/godot/pck-parser.js";
import type {
  AssetItem,
  AssetManifest,
  EngineType,
  ExtractOptions,
  ExtractionResult
} from "../types/index.js";

export class GodotAdapter extends BaseEngineAdapter {
  readonly engine: EngineType = "godot";

  async canHandle(targetPath: string): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      const stat = await fs.stat(targetPath);
      if (stat.isFile()) {
        const offsetInfo = await GodotPckParser.findPckOffset(targetPath);
        return offsetInfo !== null;
      }
      if (stat.isDirectory()) {
        const files = await fs.readdir(targetPath);
        for (const file of files) {
          if (file.toLowerCase().endsWith(".pck")) return true;
          if (file.toLowerCase().endsWith(".exe")) {
            const exePath = path.join(targetPath, file);
            const offsetInfo = await GodotPckParser.findPckOffset(exePath);
            if (offsetInfo !== null) return true;
          }
        }
      }
      return false;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Locate all PCK sources from the given target (single file or directory)
   */
  private async findPckSources(targetPath: string): Promise<string[]> {
    const stat = await fs.stat(targetPath);
    if (stat.isFile()) {
      const info = await GodotPckParser.findPckOffset(targetPath);
      return info ? [targetPath] : [];
    }

    const sources: string[] = [];
    const entries = await fs.readdir(targetPath, { withFileTypes: true });

    for (const entry of entries) {
      const fullPath = path.join(targetPath, entry.name);
      if (entry.isFile()) {
        const lower = entry.name.toLowerCase();
        if (lower.endsWith(".pck")) {
          sources.push(fullPath);
        } else if (lower.endsWith(".exe")) {
          const info = await GodotPckParser.findPckOffset(fullPath);
          if (info) sources.push(fullPath);
        }
      }
    }

    return sources;
  }

  async inspect(targetPath: string): Promise<AssetManifest> {
    const sources = await this.findPckSources(targetPath);
    if (sources.length === 0) {
      throw new Error(`No Godot PCK package found in target: ${targetPath}`);
    }

    const allAssets: AssetItem[] = [];
    let detectedEngineVersion: string | undefined;

    for (const source of sources) {
      const manifest = await GodotPckParser.readManifest(source);
      if (manifest) {
        if (!detectedEngineVersion) {
          detectedEngineVersion = `${manifest.header.engineMajor}.${manifest.header.engineMinor}.${manifest.header.enginePatch}`;
        }
        const assets = GodotPckParser.toAssetItems(manifest.files);
        allAssets.push(...assets);
      }
    }

    return this.createManifest(targetPath, allAssets, detectedEngineVersion);
  }

  async extract(targetPath: string, options: ExtractOptions): Promise<ExtractionResult> {
    const startTime = Date.now();
    const sources = await this.findPckSources(targetPath);
    if (sources.length === 0) {
      return {
        success: false,
        extractedCount: 0,
        outputDir: options.outputDir,
        durationMs: Date.now() - startTime,
        errors: [{ file: targetPath, error: "No Godot PCK package found." }]
      };
    }

    let totalExtracted = 0;
    const errors: Array<{ file: string; error: string }> = [];

    // Parse and filter assets
    for (const source of sources) {
      const manifest = await GodotPckParser.readManifest(source);
      if (!manifest) continue;

      let fileEntries = manifest.files;

      // Apply category and string filters
      if (options.categories && options.categories.length > 0) {
        const allowedCats = new Set(options.categories);
        fileEntries = fileEntries.filter((fe) => {
          const asset = GodotPckParser.toAssetItems([fe])[0];
          return allowedCats.has(asset.category);
        });
      }

      if (options.filter) {
        const f = options.filter.toLowerCase();
        fileEntries = fileEntries.filter((fe) => fe.path.toLowerCase().includes(f));
      }

      if (options.dryRun) {
        totalExtracted += fileEntries.length;
        continue;
      }

      const res = await GodotPckParser.extractFiles(source, options.outputDir, fileEntries);
      totalExtracted += res.extractedCount;
      errors.push(...res.errors);
    }

    const durationMs = Date.now() - startTime;
    return {
      success: errors.length === 0,
      extractedCount: totalExtracted,
      outputDir: options.outputDir,
      durationMs,
      errors
    };
  }
}

```

`src/adapters/index.ts`:

```ts
import type { EngineType, IEngineAdapter } from "../types/index.js";
import { EngineDetector } from "../core/detector/engine-detector.js";
import { GodotAdapter } from "./godot-adapter.js";
import { UnrealAdapter } from "./unreal-adapter.js";
import { UnityAdapter } from "./unity-adapter.js";
import { PeSoftwareAdapter } from "./pe-adapter.js";

export { BaseEngineAdapter } from "./engine-adapter.js";
export { GodotAdapter } from "./godot-adapter.js";
export { UnrealAdapter } from "./unreal-adapter.js";
export { UnityAdapter } from "./unity-adapter.js";
export { PeSoftwareAdapter } from "./pe-adapter.js";

export class AdapterRegistry {
  private static adapters: Map<EngineType, IEngineAdapter> = new Map<EngineType, IEngineAdapter>([
    ["godot", new GodotAdapter() as IEngineAdapter],
    ["unreal", new UnrealAdapter() as IEngineAdapter],
    ["unity", new UnityAdapter() as IEngineAdapter],
    ["pe-software", new PeSoftwareAdapter() as IEngineAdapter]
  ]);

  static getAdapter(engine: EngineType): IEngineAdapter | undefined {
    return this.adapters.get(engine);
  }

  /**
   * Automatically resolve the appropriate adapter for a target path
   */
  static async resolveAdapter(targetPath: string): Promise<{ adapter: IEngineAdapter; engine: EngineType }> {
    const detection = await EngineDetector.detect(targetPath);
    const adapter = this.getAdapter(detection.engine);

    if (!adapter) {
      throw new Error(
        `No adapter available for detected engine '${detection.engine}' on target: ${targetPath}`
      );
    }

    return { adapter, engine: detection.engine };
  }
}
