`src/adapters/pe-adapter.ts`:

```ts
import * as fs from "node:fs/promises";
import * as path from "node:path";
import { BaseEngineAdapter } from "./engine-adapter.js";
import { PeResourceParser, type PeInfo, type PeResourceEntry } from "../parsers/pe/pe-resource-parser.js";
import type {
  AssetCategory,
  AssetItem,
  AssetManifest,
  EngineType,
  ExtractOptions,
  ExtractionResult
} from "../types/index.js";

export class PeSoftwareAdapter extends BaseEngineAdapter {
  readonly engine: EngineType = "pe-software";

  async canHandle(targetPath: string): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      const targetExe = await this.resolveExecutablePath(targetPath);
      if (!targetExe) return false;
      const peInfo = await PeResourceParser.parse(targetExe);
      return peInfo !== null;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }

  private async resolveExecutablePath(targetPath: string): Promise<string | null> {
    const stat = await fs.stat(targetPath);
    if (stat.isFile()) {
      const ext = path.extname(targetPath).toLowerCase();
      if (ext === ".exe" || ext === ".dll") return targetPath;
      return null;
    }
    if (stat.isDirectory()) {
      const files = await fs.readdir(targetPath);
      // Look for primary .exe
      const exes = files.filter((f) => f.toLowerCase().endsWith(".exe"));
      if (exes.length > 0) {
        return path.join(targetPath, exes[0]);
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  async inspect(targetPath: string): Promise<AssetManifest> {
    const exePath = await this.resolveExecutablePath(targetPath);
    if (!exePath) {
      throw new Error(`No Windows PE executable found in: ${targetPath}`);
    }

    const peInfo = await PeResourceParser.parse(exePath);
    if (!peInfo) {
      throw new Error(`Failed to parse PE resource header in: ${exePath}`);
    }

    const assets: AssetItem[] = [];

    // Group icons represent unified .ico files
    const groupIcons = peInfo.resources.filter((r) => r.typeId === 14);
    for (const g of groupIcons) {
      assets.push({
        path: `icons/icon_${g.nameOrId}.ico`,
        category: "texture",
        size: g.size,
        metadata: { type: "RT_GROUP_ICON", nameOrId: g.nameOrId }
      });
    }

    // Process other resources
    for (const res of peInfo.resources) {
      // Skip single RT_ICON entries since they are bundled into group icons
      if (res.typeId === 3 && groupIcons.length > 0) continue;

      let category: AssetCategory = "other";
      let relPath = `resources/${res.typeName}/${res.nameOrId}`;

      switch (res.typeId) {
        case 2: // RT_BITMAP
          category = "texture";
          relPath += ".bmp";
          break;
        case 24: // RT_MANIFEST
          category = "config";
          relPath += ".xml";
          break;
        case 16: // RT_VERSION
          category = "config";
          relPath += ".json";
          break;
        case 10: // RT_RCDATA
          category = "config";
          relPath += ".rcdata";
          break;
        case 8: // RT_FONT
          category = "font";
          relPath += ".fnt";
          break;
      }

      assets.push({
        path: relPath,
        category,
        size: res.size,
        offset: res.offset,
        metadata: {
          typeId: res.typeId,
          typeName: res.typeName,
          nameOrId: res.nameOrId,
          languageId: res.languageId
        }
      });
    }

    const engineVersion = `${peInfo.framework} (${peInfo.arch})`;
    return this.createManifest(targetPath, assets, engineVersion);
  }

  async extract(targetPath: string, options: ExtractOptions): Promise<ExtractionResult> {
    const startTime = Date.now();
    const exePath = await this.resolveExecutablePath(targetPath);
    if (!exePath) {
      return {
        success: false,
        extractedCount: 0,
        outputDir: options.outputDir,
        durationMs: 0,
        errors: [{ file: targetPath, error: "No executable file found" }]
      };
    }

    const peInfo = await PeResourceParser.parse(exePath);
    if (!peInfo) {
      return {
        success: false,
        extractedCount: 0,
        outputDir: options.outputDir,
        durationMs: 0,
        errors: [{ file: exePath, error: "Failed to parse PE headers" }]
      };
    }

    let extractedCount = 0;
    const errors: Array<{ file: string; error: string }> = [];
    let fileHandle: fs.FileHandle | undefined;

    try {
      await fs.mkdir(options.outputDir, { recursive: true });
      fileHandle = await fs.open(exePath, "r");

      // 1. Extract icons as standalone .ico files
      const icons = await PeResourceParser.extractIcons(exePath, peInfo.resources);
      const iconDir = path.join(options.outputDir, "icons");
      if (icons.length > 0) {
        await fs.mkdir(iconDir, { recursive: true });
        for (const ico of icons) {
          try {
            await fs.writeFile(path.join(iconDir, ico.name), ico.buffer);
            extractedCount++;
          } catch (err: unknown) {
            errors.push({ file: ico.name, error: String(err) });
          }
        }
      }

      // 2. Extract manifests, bitmaps, RCDATA, and other resources
      for (const res of peInfo.resources) {
        // Skip RT_ICON and RT_GROUP_ICON as they were extracted above
        if (res.typeId === 3 || res.typeId === 14) continue;

        try {
          const buf = Buffer.alloc(res.size);
          await fileHandle.read(buf, 0, res.size, res.offset);

          let subDir = path.join(options.outputDir, res.typeName.toLowerCase());
          let fileName = `res_${res.nameOrId}`;

          if (res.typeId === 24) {
            // RT_MANIFEST
            subDir = path.join(options.outputDir, "manifest");
            fileName = `manifest_${res.nameOrId}.xml`;
          } else if (res.typeId === 2) {
            // RT_BITMAP
            subDir = path.join(options.outputDir, "bitmaps");
            fileName = `bitmap_${res.nameOrId}.bmp`;
            // Check if DIB header needs BITMAPFILEHEADER
            if (buf.length >= 40 && buf.readUInt32LE(0) === 40) {
              const fileHeader = Buffer.alloc(14);
              fileHeader.write("BM", 0);
              fileHeader.writeUInt32LE(14 + buf.length, 2);
              fileHeader.writeUInt32LE(14 + 40, 10);
              const bmpBuf = Buffer.concat([fileHeader, buf]);
              await fs.mkdir(subDir, { recursive: true });
              await fs.writeFile(path.join(subDir, fileName), bmpBuf);
              extractedCount++;
              continue;
            }
          } else if (res.typeId === 10) {
            // RT_RCDATA
            subDir = path.join(options.outputDir, "rcdata");
            // Check Delphi DFM magic "TPF0"
            if (buf.length >= 4 && buf.readUInt32LE(0) === 0x30465054) {
              fileName = `form_${res.nameOrId}.dfm`;
            } else if (buf.length >= 4 && buf.subarray(0, 4).equals(Buffer.from([0x89, 0x50, 0x4e, 0x47]))) {
              fileName = `image_${res.nameOrId}.png`;
            } else {
              fileName = `data_${res.nameOrId}.bin`;
            }
          }

          await fs.mkdir(subDir, { recursive: true });
          await fs.writeFile(path.join(subDir, fileName), buf);
          extractedCount++;
        } catch (err: unknown) {
          errors.push({ file: `${res.typeName}_${res.nameOrId}`, error: String(err) });
        }
      }

      return {
        success: errors.length === 0,
        extractedCount,
        outputDir: options.outputDir,
        durationMs: Date.now() - startTime,
        errors
      };
    } finally {
      if (fileHandle) {
        await fileHandle.close();
      }
    }
  }
}

```

`src/adapters/unity-adapter.ts`:

```ts
import * as fs from "node:fs/promises";
import * as path from "node:path";
import { exec } from "node:child_process";
import { promisify } from "node:util";
import { BaseEngineAdapter } from "./engine-adapter.js";
import { UnityBundleParser } from "../parsers/unity/bundle-parser.js";
import { categorizeExtension, listFilesRecursively } from "../utils/file-utils.js";
import type {
  AssetItem,
  AssetManifest,
  EngineType,
  ExtractOptions,
  ExtractionResult
} from "../types/index.js";

const execAsync = promisify(exec);

export class UnityAdapter extends BaseEngineAdapter {
  readonly engine: EngineType = "unity";

  async canHandle(targetPath: string): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      const stat = await fs.stat(targetPath);
      if (stat.isFile()) {
        const bundleInfo = await UnityBundleParser.readBundleInfo(targetPath);
        if (bundleInfo !== null) return true;
        const name = path.basename(targetPath).toLowerCase();
        if (name === "unityplayer.dll" || name === "globalgamemanagers" || name.endsWith(".assets")) {
          return true;
        }
      } else if (stat.isDirectory()) {
        const files = await fs.readdir(targetPath);
        for (const f of files) {
          const lower = f.toLowerCase();
          if (lower === "unityplayer.dll" || lower.endsWith("_data") || lower === "monobleedingedge") {
            return true;
          }
        }
      }
      return false;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }

  private async findUnityAssets(targetPath: string): Promise<string[]> {
    const stat = await fs.stat(targetPath);
    if (stat.isFile()) {
      return [targetPath];
    }
    return listFilesRecursively(targetPath, 4);
  }

  async inspect(targetPath: string): Promise<AssetManifest> {
    const files = await this.findUnityAssets(targetPath);
    const assets: AssetItem[] = [];
    let detectedVersion: string | undefined;

    for (const f of files) {
      const stat = await fs.stat(f);
      const ext = path.extname(f).toLowerCase();
      const baseName = path.basename(f).toLowerCase();
      const relPath = path.relative(targetPath, f) || baseName;

      // Check bundle header for version if applicable
      if (assets.length < 5) {
        const bundleInfo = await UnityBundleParser.readBundleInfo(f);
        if (bundleInfo && !detectedVersion) {
          detectedVersion = bundleInfo.engineVersion;
        }
      }

      let category = categorizeExtension(f);
      if (ext === ".assets" || ext === ".sharedassets" || baseName === "globalgamemanagers") {
        category = "scene";
      }

      assets.push({
        path: relPath,
        category,
        size: stat.size,
        metadata: {
          isDataFile: ext === ".assets" || ext === ".sharedassets"
        }
      });
    }

    return this.createManifest(targetPath, assets, detectedVersion);
  }

  /**
   * Check if AssetRipper CLI is available
   */
  private async isAssetRipperAvailable(): Promise<string | null> {
    const candidates = ["AssetRipper", "AssetRipper.Cli", "AssetRipper.GUI"];
    for (const cmd of candidates) {
      try {
        await execAsync(`${cmd} --version`);
        return cmd;
      } catch {
        // Continue
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  async extract(targetPath: string, options: ExtractOptions): Promise<ExtractionResult> {
    const startTime = Date.now();
    await fs.mkdir(options.outputDir, { recursive: true });

    const assetRipperCmd = await this.isAssetRipperAvailable();
    const errors: Array<{ file: string; error: string }> = [];

    if (assetRipperCmd) {
      try {
        const cmd = `${assetRipperCmd} "${targetPath}" --output "${options.outputDir}" --quiet`;
        await execAsync(cmd);
        const extracted = await listFilesRecursively(options.outputDir);
        return {
          success: true,
          extractedCount: extracted.length,
          outputDir: options.outputDir,
          durationMs: Date.now() - startTime,
          errors: []
        };
      } catch (err: unknown) {
        errors.push({
          file: targetPath,
          error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)
        });
      }
    } else {
      errors.push({
        file: targetPath,
        error: "AssetRipper CLI is not installed in PATH. Please install it via 'dotnet tool install -g AssetRipper.Cli' or download the release from https://github.com/AssetRipper/AssetRipper."
      });
    }

    return {
      success: errors.length === 0,
      extractedCount: 0,
      outputDir: options.outputDir,
      durationMs: Date.now() - startTime,
      errors
    };
  }
}

```

`src/adapters/unreal-adapter.ts`:

```ts
import * as fs from "node:fs/promises";
import * as path from "node:path";
import { exec } from "node:child_process";
import { promisify } from "node:util";
import { BaseEngineAdapter } from "./engine-adapter.js";
import { UnrealPakParser, type UnrealPakInfo } from "../parsers/unreal/pak-parser.js";
import { categorizeExtension, listFilesRecursively } from "../utils/file-utils.js";
import type {
  AssetItem,
  AssetManifest,
  EngineType,
  ExtractOptions,
  ExtractionResult
} from "../types/index.js";

const execAsync = promisify(exec);

export class UnrealAdapter extends BaseEngineAdapter {
  readonly engine: EngineType = "unreal";

  async canHandle(targetPath: string): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      const stat = await fs.stat(targetPath);
      if (stat.isFile()) {
        const ext = path.extname(targetPath).toLowerCase();
        if (ext === ".pak" || ext === ".utoc" || ext === ".ucas") return true;
        const pakInfo = await UnrealPakParser.readPakInfo(targetPath);
        return pakInfo !== null;
      }
      if (stat.isDirectory()) {
        const files = await fs.readdir(targetPath);
        for (const f of files) {
          const lower = f.toLowerCase();
          if (lower.endsWith(".pak") || lower === "engine" || lower === "content") {
            return true;
          }
        }
      }
      return false;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }

  private async findUnrealContainers(targetPath: string): Promise<string[]> {
    const stat = await fs.stat(targetPath);
    if (stat.isFile()) {
      return [targetPath];
    }

    const allFiles = await listFilesRecursively(targetPath, 4);
    return allFiles.filter((f) => {
      const ext = path.extname(f).toLowerCase();
      return ext === ".pak" || ext === ".utoc" || ext === ".ucas";
    });
  }

  async inspect(targetPath: string): Promise<AssetManifest> {
    const containers = await this.findUnrealContainers(targetPath);
    const assets: AssetItem[] = [];
    let engineVersion: string | undefined;

    for (const container of containers) {
      const stat = await fs.stat(container);
      const ext = path.extname(container).toLowerCase();
      let isEncrypted = false;

      if (ext === ".pak") {
        const info = await UnrealPakParser.readPakInfo(container);
        if (info) {
          isEncrypted = info.isEncrypted;
          if (!engineVersion) {
            engineVersion = `UE Pak v${info.version}`;
          }
        }
      } else if (ext === ".utoc" || ext === ".ucas") {
        engineVersion = "UE Zen Store (UE 4.26+ / 5)";
      }

      assets.push({
        path: path.relative(targetPath, container) || path.basename(container),
        category: "other",
        size: stat.size,
        encrypted: isEncrypted,
        metadata: {
          containerType: ext.toUpperCase().replace(".", ""),
          isContainerPackage: true
        }
      });
    }

    return this.createManifest(targetPath, assets, engineVersion);
  }

  /**
   * Check if repak CLI is available in PATH
   */
  private async isRepakAvailable(): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      await execAsync("repak --version");
      return true;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }

  async extract(targetPath: string, options: ExtractOptions): Promise<ExtractionResult> {
    const startTime = Date.now();
    const containers = await this.findUnrealContainers(targetPath);
    const errors: Array<{ file: string; error: string }> = [];
    let extractedCount = 0;

    await fs.mkdir(options.outputDir, { recursive: true });
    const hasRepak = await this.isRepakAvailable();

    for (const container of containers) {
      const ext = path.extname(container).toLowerCase();
      if (ext === ".pak" && hasRepak) {
        try {
          const keyArg = options.aesKey ? `-k "${options.aesKey}"` : "";
          const cmd = `repak unpack ${keyArg} "${container}" -o "${options.outputDir}"`;
          await execAsync(cmd);
          extractedCount++;
        } catch (err: unknown) {
          errors.push({
            file: container,
            error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)
          });
        }
      } else {
        // Fallback or tool notification
        if (!hasRepak) {
          errors.push({
            file: container,
            error: "repak CLI is not installed in PATH. Install via 'cargo install repak' to unpack encrypted/compressed Unreal .pak containers."
          });
        }
      }
    }

    return {
      success: errors.length === 0 && extractedCount > 0,
      extractedCount,
      outputDir: options.outputDir,
      durationMs: Date.now() - startTime,
      errors
    };
  }
}

```

`src/cli/index.ts`:

```ts
import { Command } from "commander";
import chalk from "chalk";
import ora from "ora";
import * as path from "node:path";
import { EngineDetector } from "../core/detector/engine-detector.js";
import { AdapterRegistry } from "../adapters/index.js";
import { ReaBridge } from "../rea/rea-bridge.js";
import { runMcpServer } from "../mcp/server.js";
import type { AssetCategory } from "../types/index.js";

const program = new Command();

program
  .name("ripper")
  .description("Universal Asset Ripper - Multi-engine game asset extractor & REA orchestrator")
  .version("0.1.0");

// Command: detect
program
  .command("detect <target>")
  .description("Detect the game engine (Unity, Unreal, Godot) and analyze binary/container signatures")
  .action(async (target: string) => {
    const spinner = ora(`Analyzing target: ${chalk.cyan(target)}...`).start();
    try {
      const resolved = path.resolve(target);
      const result = await EngineDetector.detect(resolved);
      spinner.succeed(`Engine analysis completed.`);

      console.log();
      console.log(chalk.bold("────────────────────────────────────────"));
      console.log(chalk.bold.green("  Engine Detection Results"));
      console.log(chalk.bold("────────────────────────────────────────"));
      console.log(`  Engine:         ${chalk.yellow.bold(result.engine.toUpperCase())}`);
      console.log(`  Confidence:     ${chalk.cyan(Math.round(result.confidence * 100))}%`);
      if (result.version) {
        console.log(`  Version:        ${chalk.white(result.version)}`);
      }
      if (result.containerType) {
        console.log(`  Container:      ${chalk.white(result.containerType)}`);
      }
      if (result.arch && result.arch !== "unknown") {
        console.log(`  Architecture:   ${chalk.white(result.arch)}`);
      }
      if (result.details.isIL2CPP) {
        console.log(`  Runtime:        ${chalk.magenta("IL2CPP (Native AOT)")}`);
      } else if (result.details.isMono) {
        console.log(`  Runtime:        ${chalk.magenta("Mono (.NET CIL)")}`);
      }
      if (result.details.encrypted !== undefined) {
        console.log(`  Encrypted:      ${result.details.encrypted ? chalk.red("Yes") : chalk.green("No")}`);
      }

      if (result.details.markersFound.length > 0) {
        console.log();
        console.log(chalk.bold("  Signatures & Markers Found:"));
        for (const marker of result.details.markersFound) {
          console.log(`   ${chalk.green("✔")} ${marker}`);
        }
      }

      if (result.details.notes && result.details.notes.length > 0) {
        console.log();
        console.log(chalk.bold("  Notes:"));
        for (const note of result.details.notes) {
          console.log(`   ${chalk.dim("ℹ")} ${note}`);
        }
      }
      console.log(chalk.bold("────────────────────────────────────────"));
      console.log();
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      spinner.fail(`Detection failed: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`);
      process.exit(1);
    }
  });

// Command: inspect
program
  .command("inspect <target>")
  .description("Inspect game assets, listing counts and total sizes by category")
  .action(async (target: string) => {
    const spinner = ora(`Inspecting package: ${chalk.cyan(target)}...`).start();
    try {
      const resolved = path.resolve(target);
      const { adapter, engine } = await AdapterRegistry.resolveAdapter(resolved);
      const manifest = await adapter.inspect(resolved);
      spinner.succeed(`Inspection complete for ${chalk.yellow(engine.toUpperCase())}.`);

      console.log();
      console.log(chalk.bold("────────────────────────────────────────"));
      console.log(chalk.bold.green(`  Asset Manifest: ${chalk.white(path.basename(resolved))}`));
      console.log(chalk.bold("────────────────────────────────────────"));
      console.log(`  Engine:         ${chalk.yellow(manifest.engine.toUpperCase())}`);
      if (manifest.engineVersion) {
        console.log(`  Engine Version: ${chalk.white(manifest.engineVersion)}`);
      }
      console.log(`  Total Files:    ${chalk.cyan(manifest.totalFiles.toLocaleString())}`);
      console.log(`  Total Size:     ${chalk.cyan((manifest.totalBytes / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2))} MB`);
      console.log();
      console.log(chalk.bold("  Assets Breakdown by Category:"));
      console.log(chalk.dim("  ┌──────────────┬────────────┬──────────────┐"));
      console.log(chalk.dim("  │ Category     │ Files      │ Size (MB)    │"));
      console.log(chalk.dim("  ├──────────────┼────────────┼──────────────┤"));

      for (const [category, summary] of Object.entries(manifest.byCategory)) {
        if (summary.count > 0) {
          const catPadded = category.padEnd(12);
          const countPadded = summary.count.toString().padStart(10);
          const sizeMb = (summary.totalBytes / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(2).padStart(12);
          console.log(`  │ ${chalk.cyan(catPadded)} │ ${countPadded} │ ${sizeMb} │`);
        }
      }
      console.log(chalk.dim("  └──────────────┴────────────┴──────────────┘"));
      console.log();
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      spinner.fail(`Inspection failed: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`);
      process.exit(1);
    }
  });

// Command: extract
program
  .command("extract <target>")
  .description("Extract game assets to an output directory")
  .requiredOption("-o, --output <dir>", "Output directory for extracted assets")
  .option("--categories <cats>", "Comma-separated categories to extract (texture,mesh,audio,scene,script,shader)")
  .option("--filter <pattern>", "Filter files containing this substring")
  .option("--aes-key <hex>", "AES-256 decryption key (Unreal Engine)")
  .option("--dry-run", "Simulate extraction without writing files to disk")
  .action(async (target: string, opts: { output: string; categories?: string; filter?: string; aesKey?: string; dryRun?: boolean }) => {
    const spinner = ora(`Extracting assets from ${chalk.cyan(target)}...`).start();
    try {
      const resolved = path.resolve(target);
      const outDir = path.resolve(opts.output);
      const { adapter, engine } = await AdapterRegistry.resolveAdapter(resolved);

      const categories = opts.categories
        ? (opts.categories.split(",").map((c) => c.trim()) as AssetCategory[])
        : undefined;

      const result = await adapter.extract(resolved, {
        outputDir: outDir,
        categories,
        filter: opts.filter,
        aesKey: opts.aesKey,
        dryRun: opts.dryRun
      });

      if (result.success) {
        spinner.succeed(
          `Extracted ${chalk.green(result.extractedCount)} files to ${chalk.cyan(outDir)} in ${result.durationMs}ms.`
        );
      } else {
        spinner.warn(
          `Extraction finished with warnings. Extracted: ${result.extractedCount} files.`
        );
        if (result.errors.length > 0) {
          console.log();
          console.log(chalk.bold.red("  Errors/Warnings:"));
          for (const err of result.errors.slice(0, 10)) {
            console.log(`   ${chalk.red("✖")} [${err.file}] ${err.error}`);
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      spinner.fail(`Extraction failed: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`);
      process.exit(1);
    }
  });

// Command: rea-inspect
program
  .command("rea-inspect <binary>")
  .description("Run deep binary reverse engineering scan (AES keys, IL2CPP metadata, and REA evidence bundle)")
  .action(async (binary: string) => {
    const spinner = ora(`Running REA deep binary scan on: ${chalk.cyan(binary)}...`).start();
    try {
      const resolved = path.resolve(binary);
      const { scan, bundle } = await ReaBridge.inspectBinary(resolved);
      spinner.succeed(`Binary inspection complete.`);

      console.log();
      console.log(chalk.bold("────────────────────────────────────────"));
      console.log(chalk.bold.magenta("  REA Binary Analysis & Evidence Bundle"));
      console.log(chalk.bold("────────────────────────────────────────"));
      console.log(`  Binary:         ${chalk.white(path.basename(resolved))}`);
      console.log(`  Architecture:   ${chalk.white(scan.arch)}`);

      if (scan.candidateAesKeys.length > 0) {
        console.log();
        console.log(chalk.bold.yellow("  Candidate AES-256 Keys Discovered:"));
        for (const key of scan.candidateAesKeys) {
          console.log(`   ${chalk.green("🔑")} ${chalk.bold(key.keyHex)} (Offset: 0x${key.offset.toString(16)}, Entropy: ${key.entropy.toFixed(2)}, Conf: ${key.confidence})`);
        }
      } else {
        console.log(`  AES Keys:       ${chalk.dim("No plaintext 256-bit keys detected")}`);
      }

      if (scan.il2cppMetadata) {
        console.log();
        console.log(chalk.bold.cyan("  IL2CPP Metadata Detected:"));
        console.log(`   Version: ${scan.il2cppMetadata.version}`);
      }

      if (scan.stringsFound.length > 0) {
        console.log();
        console.log(chalk.bold("  Signatures & Symbols Found:"));
        for (const s of scan.stringsFound) {
          console.log(`   ${chalk.cyan("✔")} ${s}`);
        }
      }

      if (scan.recommendations.length > 0) {
        console.log();
        console.log(chalk.bold("  Recommendations for REA Agents:"));
        for (const rec of scan.recommendations) {
          console.log(`   ${chalk.yellow("⚡")} ${rec}`);
        }
      }

      console.log(chalk.bold("────────────────────────────────────────"));
      console.log();
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      spinner.fail(`Binary inspection failed: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`);
      process.exit(1);
    }
  });

// Command: mcp
program
  .command("mcp")
  .description("Start the Model Context Protocol (MCP) server over standard I/O")
  .action(async () => {
    await runMcpServer();
  });

program.parse(process.argv);

```

`src/core/detector/engine-detector.ts`:

```ts
import * as fs from "node:fs/promises";
import * as path from "node:path";
import type { EngineDetectionResult, EngineDetails, EngineType } from "../../types/index.js";
import { listFilesRecursively, parsePeHeaderArch } from "../../utils/file-utils.js";
import { GodotPckParser } from "../../parsers/godot/pck-parser.js";
import { UnrealPakParser } from "../../parsers/unreal/pak-parser.js";
import { UnityBundleParser } from "../../parsers/unity/bundle-parser.js";
import { PeResourceParser } from "../../parsers/pe/pe-resource-parser.js";

export class EngineDetector {
  /**
   * Main entry point to detect game engine from a folder or file path
   */
  static async detect(targetPath: string): Promise<EngineDetectionResult> {
    const stat = await fs.stat(targetPath);
    if (stat.isFile()) {
      return this.detectSingleFile(targetPath);
    } else if (stat.isDirectory()) {
      return this.detectDirectory(targetPath);
    }

    return {
      engine: "unknown",
      confidence: 0,
      targetPath,
      details: {
        markersFound: [],
        detectedFiles: []
      }
    };
  }

  /**
   * Detect engine when given a single file (.exe, .pak, .pck, .bundle, etc.)
   */
  static async detectSingleFile(filePath: string): Promise<EngineDetectionResult> {
    const ext = path.extname(filePath).toLowerCase();
    const fileName = path.basename(filePath).toLowerCase();
    const markersFound: string[] = [];
    const detectedFiles: string[] = [filePath];
    const arch = await parsePeHeaderArch(filePath);

    // 1. Check Godot (PCK or embedded in EXE)
    const godotPck = await GodotPckParser.readManifest(filePath);
    if (godotPck) {
      markersFound.push(godotPck.header.isEmbedded ? "Godot Embedded GDPC Header" : "Godot Standalone PCK");
      const versionStr = `${godotPck.header.engineMajor}.${godotPck.header.engineMinor}.${godotPck.header.enginePatch}`;
      return {
        engine: "godot",
        confidence: 0.99,
        version: versionStr,
        arch,
        containerType: godotPck.header.isEmbedded ? "Embedded PCK" : "Standalone PCK",
        targetPath: filePath,
        details: {
          markersFound,
          detectedFiles,
          notes: [
            `Format Version: ${godotPck.header.formatVersion}`,
            `Files indexed: ${godotPck.files.length}`,
            `Embedded in executable: ${godotPck.header.isEmbedded}`
          ]
        }
      };
    }

    // 2. Check Unreal Engine (.pak)
    if (ext === ".pak") {
      const pakInfo = await UnrealPakParser.readPakInfo(filePath);
      markersFound.push("Unreal .pak container");
      if (pakInfo) {
        markersFound.push(`Unreal FPakInfo (v${pakInfo.version})`);
        return {
          engine: "unreal",
          confidence: 0.95,
          version: `UE Pak v${pakInfo.version}`,
          containerType: "Unreal PAK",
          targetPath: filePath,
          details: {
            markersFound,
            detectedFiles,
            encrypted: pakInfo.isEncrypted,
            notes: [`Encrypted index: ${pakInfo.isEncrypted}`, `Index offset: ${pakInfo.indexOffset}`]
          }
        };
      }
      return {
        engine: "unreal",
        confidence: 0.85,
        containerType: "Unreal PAK",
        targetPath: filePath,
        details: { markersFound, detectedFiles }
      };
    }

    // 3. Check Unreal Zen Store containers (.utoc, .ucas)
    if (ext === ".utoc" || ext === ".ucas") {
      markersFound.push("Unreal Zen Store IoStore container");
      return {
        engine: "unreal",
        confidence: 0.95,
        version: "Unreal Engine 4.26+ / 5",
        containerType: "IoStore (.utoc/.ucas)",
        targetPath: filePath,
        details: {
          markersFound,
          detectedFiles,
          notes: ["Zen Store format requires IoStore reader"]
        }
      };
    }

    // 4. Check Unity Bundle / Serialized file
    const unityBundle = await UnityBundleParser.readBundleInfo(filePath);
    if (unityBundle) {
      markersFound.push(`Unity Container (${unityBundle.signature})`);
      return {
        engine: "unity",
        confidence: 0.95,
        version: unityBundle.engineVersion,
        containerType: unityBundle.signature,
        targetPath: filePath,
        details: {
          markersFound,
          detectedFiles,
          notes: [`Format Version: ${unityBundle.formatVersion}`]
        }
      };
    }

    // 5. Check if it's a Unity or Unreal binary executable
    if (ext === ".exe" || ext === ".dll") {
      if (fileName === "unityplayer.dll") {
        return {
          engine: "unity",
          confidence: 1.0,
          arch,
          targetPath: filePath,
          details: { markersFound: ["UnityPlayer.dll binary"], detectedFiles }
        };
      }
      if (fileName === "gameassembly.dll") {
        return {
          engine: "unity",
          confidence: 1.0,
          arch,
          targetPath: filePath,
          details: {
            isIL2CPP: true,
            markersFound: ["GameAssembly.dll (IL2CPP)"],
            detectedFiles
          }
        };
      }

      // Check generic Windows PE software executable
      const peInfo = await PeResourceParser.parse(filePath);
      if (peInfo) {
        markersFound.push(`Windows PE Binary (${peInfo.arch})`);
        markersFound.push(`Framework: ${peInfo.framework}`);
        if (peInfo.resources.length > 0) {
          markersFound.push(`PE Resource Directory: ${peInfo.resources.length} resource entries`);
        }
        return {
          engine: "pe-software",
          confidence: 0.95,
          version: peInfo.framework,
          arch: peInfo.arch,
          containerType: `Windows PE (${peInfo.framework})`,
          targetPath: filePath,
          details: {
            framework: peInfo.framework,
            markersFound,
            detectedFiles,
            notes: [
              `Sections: ${peInfo.sections.map((s) => s.name).join(", ")}`,
              `Total Resources: ${peInfo.resources.length}`
            ]
          }
        };
      }
    }

    // Fallback: check sibling files if single file is in a folder
    try {
      const parentDir = path.dirname(filePath);
      const parentDetection = await this.detectDirectory(parentDir, 2);
      if (parentDetection.engine !== "unknown") {
        return {
          ...parentDetection,
          targetPath: filePath,
          details: {
            ...parentDetection.details,
            detectedFiles: [filePath, ...parentDetection.details.detectedFiles]
          }
        };
      }
    } catch {
      // Ignore
    }

    return {
      engine: "unknown",
      confidence: 0.1,
      arch,
      targetPath: filePath,
      details: {
        markersFound,
        detectedFiles
      }
    };
  }

  /**
   * Detect engine by scanning a game root directory
   */
  static async detectDirectory(dirPath: string, maxDepth = 4): Promise<EngineDetectionResult> {
    const entries = await fs.readdir(dirPath, { withFileTypes: true });
    const markersFound: string[] = [];
    const detectedFiles: string[] = [];
    let detectedArch: "x86" | "x64" | "arm64" | "unknown" = "unknown";

    let unityScore = 0;
    let unrealScore = 0;
    let godotScore = 0;

    let isIL2CPP = false;
    let isMono = false;
    let detectedVersion: string | undefined;
    let detectedContainer: string | undefined;
    let isEncrypted = false;

    // Check direct entries
    for (const entry of entries) {
      const lower = entry.name.toLowerCase();
      const fullPath = path.join(dirPath, entry.name);

      if (entry.isFile()) {
        detectedFiles.push(entry.name);

        // Check PE arch from exe
        if (lower.endsWith(".exe") && detectedArch === "unknown") {
          detectedArch = await parsePeHeaderArch(fullPath);
        }

        // --- UNITY CHECKS ---
        if (lower === "unityplayer.dll") {
          unityScore += 50;
          markersFound.push("UnityPlayer.dll");
        }
        if (lower === "gameassembly.dll") {
          unityScore += 40;
          isIL2CPP = true;
          markersFound.push("GameAssembly.dll (IL2CPP runtime)");
        }
        if (lower === "data.unity3d") {
          unityScore += 30;
          markersFound.push("data.unity3d");
        }

        // --- UNREAL CHECKS ---
        if (lower.endsWith(".pak")) {
          unrealScore += 35;
          markersFound.push(`Unreal PAK: ${entry.name}`);
          detectedContainer = "PAK";
          const pakInfo = await UnrealPakParser.readPakInfo(fullPath);
          if (pakInfo?.isEncrypted) {
            isEncrypted = true;
          }
        }
        if (lower.endsWith(".utoc") || lower.endsWith(".ucas")) {
          unrealScore += 40;
          markersFound.push(`Unreal Zen Store: ${entry.name}`);
          detectedContainer = "IoStore (.utoc/.ucas)";
          detectedVersion = "Unreal Engine 5 / 4.26+";
        }

        // --- GODOT CHECKS ---
        if (lower.endsWith(".pck")) {
          godotScore += 50;
          markersFound.push(`Godot PCK: ${entry.name}`);
          detectedContainer = "PCK";
          const pckManifest = await GodotPckParser.readManifest(fullPath);
          if (pckManifest) {
            detectedVersion = `${pckManifest.header.engineMajor}.${pckManifest.header.engineMinor}.${pckManifest.header.enginePatch}`;
            markersFound.push(`Godot PCK v${pckManifest.header.formatVersion} (Engine ${detectedVersion})`);
          }
        }

        // Check embedded Godot in .exe
        if (lower.endsWith(".exe")) {
          const embeddedPck = await GodotPckParser.readManifest(fullPath);
          if (embeddedPck) {
            godotScore += 60;
            detectedContainer = "Embedded PCK in EXE";
            detectedVersion = `${embeddedPck.header.engineMajor}.${embeddedPck.header.engineMinor}.${embeddedPck.header.enginePatch}`;
            markersFound.push(`Godot Executable with embedded PCK (Engine ${detectedVersion})`);
          }
        }
      } else if (entry.isDirectory()) {
        // --- UNITY FOLDER CHECKS ---
        if (lower.endsWith("_data")) {
          unityScore += 40;
          markersFound.push(`Unity Data Folder: ${entry.name}`);

          // Look inside _Data folder
          try {
            const dataFiles = await fs.readdir(fullPath);
            for (const df of dataFiles) {
              const dfLower = df.toLowerCase();
              if (dfLower === "globalgamemanagers") {
                unityScore += 20;
                markersFound.push("globalgamemanagers");
              }
              if (dfLower === "managed") {
                unityScore += 15;
                isMono = true;
                markersFound.push("Mono Managed folder");
              }
              if (dfLower === "il2cpp_data") {
                unityScore += 25;
                isIL2CPP = true;
                markersFound.push("il2cpp_data directory");
              }
              if (dfLower.endsWith(".assets")) {
                unityScore += 10;
              }
            }
          } catch {
            // Ignore read errors
          }
        }

        if (lower === "monobleedingedge") {
          unityScore += 25;
          isMono = true;
          markersFound.push("MonoBleedingEdge runtime");
        }

        // --- UNREAL FOLDER CHECKS ---
        if (lower === "engine") {
          unrealScore += 25;
          markersFound.push("Engine directory");
        }
        if (lower === "content" || lower === "paks") {
          unrealScore += 25;
          markersFound.push(`${entry.name} directory`);
          try {
            const subEntries = await listFilesRecursively(fullPath, 3);
            for (const cf of subEntries) {
              const cfLower = cf.toLowerCase();
              if (cfLower.endsWith(".pak")) {
                unrealScore += 40;
                markersFound.push(`Unreal PAK: ${path.basename(cf)}`);
                detectedContainer = "PAK";
                const pakInfo = await UnrealPakParser.readPakInfo(cf);
                if (pakInfo?.isEncrypted) isEncrypted = true;
                break;
              }
              if (cfLower.endsWith(".utoc") || cfLower.endsWith(".ucas")) {
                unrealScore += 45;
                markersFound.push(`Unreal Zen Store: ${path.basename(cf)}`);
                detectedContainer = "IoStore (.utoc/.ucas)";
                detectedVersion = "Unreal Engine 5 / 4.26+";
                break;
              }
            }
          } catch {
            // Ignore
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // Recursive search in subdirectories if scores are low
    if (unityScore === 0 && unrealScore === 0 && godotScore === 0 && maxDepth > 1) {
      try {
        const subdirs = entries.filter((e) => e.isDirectory()).map((e) => path.join(dirPath, e.name));
        for (const sub of subdirs) {
          const subResult = await this.detectDirectory(sub, maxDepth - 1);
          if (subResult.engine !== "unknown") {
            return subResult;
          }
        }
      } catch {
        // Ignore
      }
    }

    // Determine winning engine
    let engine: EngineType = "unknown";
    let confidence = 0;

    if (unityScore > unrealScore && unityScore > godotScore && unityScore >= 20) {
      engine = "unity";
      confidence = Math.min(1.0, unityScore / 80);
      detectedContainer = detectedContainer ?? (isIL2CPP ? "IL2CPP Assets" : "Mono Assets");
    } else if (unrealScore > unityScore && unrealScore > godotScore && unrealScore >= 20) {
      engine = "unreal";
      confidence = Math.min(1.0, unrealScore / 80);
      detectedContainer = detectedContainer ?? "Unreal Content / PAK";
    } else if (godotScore > unityScore && godotScore > unrealScore && godotScore >= 20) {
      engine = "godot";
      confidence = Math.min(1.0, godotScore / 70);
      detectedContainer = detectedContainer ?? "PCK Archive";
    }

    // Check for Windows software application (PE executable) if no game engine matched
    if (engine === "unknown") {
      const exeFiles = entries.filter((e) => e.isFile() && e.name.toLowerCase().endsWith(".exe"));
      if (exeFiles.length > 0) {
        const primaryExe = path.join(dirPath, exeFiles[0].name);
        const peInfo = await PeResourceParser.parse(primaryExe);
        if (peInfo) {
          markersFound.push(`Windows PE Software Executable: ${exeFiles[0].name}`);
          markersFound.push(`Framework: ${peInfo.framework}`);
          if (peInfo.resources.length > 0) {
            markersFound.push(`PE Resource Directory: ${peInfo.resources.length} resources`);
          }
          return {
            engine: "pe-software",
            confidence: 0.95,
            version: peInfo.framework,
            arch: peInfo.arch,
            containerType: `Windows PE (${peInfo.framework})`,
            targetPath: dirPath,
            details: {
              framework: peInfo.framework,
              markersFound,
              detectedFiles: detectedFiles.slice(0, 50),
              notes: [
                `Primary Executable: ${exeFiles[0].name}`,
                `Sections: ${peInfo.sections.map((s) => s.name).join(", ")}`,
                `Resources found: ${peInfo.resources.length}`
              ]
            }
          };
        }
      }
    }

    return {
      engine,
      confidence,
      version: detectedVersion,
      arch: detectedArch,
      containerType: detectedContainer,
      targetPath: dirPath,
      details: {
        isIL2CPP,
        isMono,
        encrypted: isEncrypted,
        markersFound,
        detectedFiles: detectedFiles.slice(0, 50)
      }
    };
  }
}
```

`src/mcp/server.ts`:

```ts
import { Server } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/index.js";
import { StdioServerTransport } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/stdio.js";
import {
  CallToolRequestSchema,
  ListToolsRequestSchema
} from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/types.js";
import { EngineDetector } from "../core/detector/engine-detector.js";
import { AdapterRegistry } from "../adapters/index.js";
import { ReaBridge } from "../rea/rea-bridge.js";
import type { AssetCategory } from "../types/index.js";

export function createMcpServer(): Server {
  const server = new Server(
    {
      name: "universal-asset-ripper",
      version: "0.1.0"
    },
    {
      capabilities: {
        tools: {}
      }
    }
  );

  // List available tools
  server.setRequestHandler(ListToolsRequestSchema, async () => {
    return {
      tools: [
        {
          name: "ripper_detect",
          description: "Detect the game engine (Unity, Unreal Engine, Godot) or Windows desktop software (PE, Delphi, .NET, Electron).",
          inputSchema: {
            type: "object",
            properties: {
              targetPath: {
                type: "string",
                description: "Path to the target directory or executable (.exe, .pck, .pak, .bundle)"
              }
            },
            required: ["targetPath"]
          }
        },
        {
          name: "ripper_inspect",
          description: "Inspect game assets, listing counts and sizes by category (textures, meshes, audio, scenes, scripts).",
          inputSchema: {
            type: "object",
            properties: {
              targetPath: {
                type: "string",
                description: "Path to the game folder or asset container"
              }
            },
            required: ["targetPath"]
          }
        },
        {
          name: "ripper_extract",
          description: "Extract assets from game package with optional category filtering and AES key support.",
          inputSchema: {
            type: "object",
            properties: {
              targetPath: {
                type: "string",
                description: "Path to the game folder or container"
              },
              outputDir: {
                type: "string",
                description: "Directory where extracted assets should be saved"
              },
              categories: {
                type: "array",
                items: {
                  type: "string",
                  enum: [
                    "texture",
                    "mesh",
                    "audio",
                    "scene",
                    "script",
                    "shader",
                    "font",
                    "video",
                    "animation",
                    "config",
                    "other"
                  ]
                },
                description: "Optional list of asset categories to extract"
              },
              filter: {
                type: "string",
                description: "Substring filter to match asset paths"
              },
              aesKey: {
                type: "string",
                description: "AES-256 decryption key for encrypted Unreal .pak containers"
              }
            },
            required: ["targetPath", "outputDir"]
          }
        },
        {
          name: "ripper_scan_binary",
          description: "Scan an executable or binary for candidate AES keys, IL2CPP metadata, and REA evidence bundle.",
          inputSchema: {
            type: "object",
            properties: {
              binaryPath: {
                type: "string",
                description: "Path to the game executable or binary (.exe, .dll, .so)"
              }
            },
            required: ["binaryPath"]
          }
        }
      ]
    };
  });

  // Handle tool calls
  server.setRequestHandler(CallToolRequestSchema, async (request) => {
    const { name, arguments: args } = request.params;

    try {
      if (name === "ripper_detect") {
        const targetPath = String(args?.targetPath);
        const result = await EngineDetector.detect(targetPath);
        return {
          content: [
            {
              type: "text",
              text: JSON.stringify(result, null, 2)
            }
          ]
        };
      }

      if (name === "ripper_inspect") {
        const targetPath = String(args?.targetPath);
        const { adapter } = await AdapterRegistry.resolveAdapter(targetPath);
        const manifest = await adapter.inspect(targetPath);
        return {
          content: [
            {
              type: "text",
              text: JSON.stringify(manifest, null, 2)
            }
          ]
        };
      }

      if (name === "ripper_extract") {
        const targetPath = String(args?.targetPath);
        const outputDir = String(args?.outputDir);
        const categories = args?.categories as AssetCategory[] | undefined;
        const filter = args?.filter ? String(args.filter) : undefined;
        const aesKey = args?.aesKey ? String(args.aesKey) : undefined;

        const { adapter } = await AdapterRegistry.resolveAdapter(targetPath);
        const result = await adapter.extract(targetPath, {
          outputDir,
          categories,
          filter,
          aesKey
        });

        return {
          content: [
            {
              type: "text",
              text: JSON.stringify(result, null, 2)
            }
          ]
        };
      }

      if (name === "ripper_scan_binary") {
        const binaryPath = String(args?.binaryPath);
        const inspection = await ReaBridge.inspectBinary(binaryPath);
        return {
          content: [
            {
              type: "text",
              text: JSON.stringify(inspection, null, 2)
            }
          ]
        };
      }

      throw new Error(`Unknown tool: ${name}`);
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      return {
        isError: true,
        content: [
          {
            type: "text",
            text: `Error executing ${name}: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`
          }
        ]
      };
    }
  });

  return server;
}

export async function runMcpServer(): Promise<void> {
  const server = createMcpServer();
  const transport = new StdioServerTransport();
  await server.connect(transport);
}
```

`src/parsers/godot/pck-parser.ts`:

```ts
import * as fs from "node:fs/promises";
import * as path from "node:path";
import { categorizeExtension } from "../../utils/file-utils.js";
import type { AssetItem } from "../../types/index.js";

export const GDPC_MAGIC = 0x43504447; // "GDPC" in Little Endian

export interface PckHeader {
  magic: number;
  formatVersion: number;
  engineMajor: number;
  engineMinor: number;
  enginePatch: number;
  flags: number;
  filesBaseOffset: bigint;
  fileCount: number;
  isEmbedded: boolean;
  pckStartOffset: bigint;
}

export interface PckFileEntry {
  path: string;
  offset: bigint;
  size: bigint;
  md5: string;
  flags: number;
}

export class GodotPckParser {
  /**
   * Find PCK start offset in a file (standalone or embedded in an executable)
   */
  static async findPckOffset(filePath: string): Promise<{ offset: bigint; isEmbedded: boolean } | null> {
    let fileHandle: fs.FileHandle | undefined;
    try {
      fileHandle = await fs.open(filePath, "r");
      const stat = await fileHandle.stat();
      if (stat.size < 16) return null;

      // 1. Check if starts with GDPC
      const headBuf = Buffer.alloc(4);
      await fileHandle.read(headBuf, 0, 4, 0);
      if (headBuf.readUInt32LE(0) === GDPC_MAGIC) {
        return { offset: 0n, isEmbedded: false };
      }

      // 2. Check footer for embedded PCK
      // Godot embeds: [PCK content] ... [8 bytes offset to PCK] [4 bytes GDPC magic] (total 12 bytes footer)
      const footerSize = Math.min(64, Number(stat.size));
      const footBuf = Buffer.alloc(footerSize);
      await fileHandle.read(footBuf, 0, footerSize, stat.size - footerSize);

      // Search for magic GDPC near the end
      for (let i = footerSize - 4; i >= 0; i--) {
        if (footBuf.readUInt32LE(i) === GDPC_MAGIC) {
          // In standard Godot binaries, 8 bytes before magic holds int64 offset to PCK
          if (i >= 8) {
            const pckOffset = footBuf.readBigInt64LE(i - 8);
            if (pckOffset >= 0n && pckOffset < BigInt(stat.size)) {
              // Verify that at pckOffset, there is GDPC
              const verifyBuf = Buffer.alloc(4);
              await fileHandle.read(verifyBuf, 0, 4, Number(pckOffset));
              if (verifyBuf.readUInt32LE(0) === GDPC_MAGIC) {
                return { offset: pckOffset, isEmbedded: true };
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }

      return null;
    } catch {
      return null;
    } finally {
      if (fileHandle) {
        await fileHandle.close();
      }
    }
  }

  /**
   * Read and parse the PCK header & index
   */
  static async readManifest(filePath: string): Promise<{ header: PckHeader; files: PckFileEntry[] } | null> {
    const loc = await this.findPckOffset(filePath);
    if (!loc) return null;

    let fileHandle: fs.FileHandle | undefined;
    try {
      fileHandle = await fs.open(filePath, "r");
      let currentOffset = loc.offset;

      // Read initial header block (magic + format + versions = 20 bytes)
      const headerBuf = Buffer.alloc(128);
      await fileHandle.read(headerBuf, 0, 128, Number(currentOffset));

      const magic = headerBuf.readUInt32LE(0);
      if (magic !== GDPC_MAGIC) return null;

      const formatVersion = headerBuf.readUInt32LE(4);
      const engineMajor = headerBuf.readUInt32LE(8);
      const engineMinor = headerBuf.readUInt32LE(12);
      const enginePatch = headerBuf.readUInt32LE(16);

      let flags = 0;
      let filesBaseOffset = 0n;
      let cursor = 20;

      if (formatVersion >= 2) {
        flags = headerBuf.readUInt32LE(cursor);
        cursor += 4;
        filesBaseOffset = headerBuf.readBigInt64LE(cursor);
        cursor += 8;
      }
      // In Godot PCK format, 16 reserved uint32 (64 bytes) follow
      cursor += 64;

      // Read file count
      const countBuf = Buffer.alloc(4);
      await fileHandle.read(countBuf, 0, 4, Number(currentOffset) + cursor);
      cursor += 4;
      const fileCount = countBuf.readUInt32LE(0);

      const files: PckFileEntry[] = [];
      let indexOffset = Number(currentOffset) + cursor;

      // Read all file entries
      for (let i = 0; i < fileCount; i++) {
        const lenBuf = Buffer.alloc(4);
        await fileHandle.read(lenBuf, 0, 4, indexOffset);
        indexOffset += 4;
        const pathLen = lenBuf.readUInt32LE(0);

        if (pathLen > 4096 || pathLen === 0) {
          break; // Safeguard against malformed index
        }

        // Align pathLen to 4 bytes if padded, or read exact
        const pathBuf = Buffer.alloc(pathLen);
        await fileHandle.read(pathBuf, 0, pathLen, indexOffset);
        indexOffset += pathLen;
        // Godot strings in PCK might have null terminator or 4-byte alignment
        const nullIdx = pathBuf.indexOf(0);
        const entryPath = pathBuf.subarray(0, nullIdx >= 0 ? nullIdx : pathLen).toString("utf-8");

        const metaBuf = Buffer.alloc(formatVersion >= 2 ? 36 : 32);
        // Offset: 8 bytes (int64)
        // Size: 8 bytes (int64)
        // MD5: 16 bytes
        // Flags: 4 bytes (v2+)
        await fileHandle.read(metaBuf, 0, metaBuf.length, indexOffset);
        indexOffset += metaBuf.length;

        const fileOffset = metaBuf.readBigInt64LE(0);
        const fileSize = metaBuf.readBigInt64LE(8);
        const md5 = metaBuf.subarray(16, 32).toString("hex");
        const fileFlags = formatVersion >= 2 ? metaBuf.readUInt32LE(32) : 0;

        files.push({
          path: entryPath,
          offset: fileOffset,
          size: fileSize,
          md5,
          flags: fileFlags
        });
      }

      return {
        header: {
          magic,
          formatVersion,
          engineMajor,
          engineMinor,
          enginePatch,
          flags,
          filesBaseOffset,
          fileCount: files.length,
          isEmbedded: loc.isEmbedded,
          pckStartOffset: loc.offset
        },
        files
      };
    } catch {
      return null;
    } finally {
      if (fileHandle) {
        await fileHandle.close();
      }
    }
  }

  /**
   * Convert PCK files list into standard AssetItem array
   */
  static toAssetItems(files: PckFileEntry[]): AssetItem[] {
    return files.map((file) => {
      // Clean 'res://' prefix
      const cleanPath = file.path.replace(/^res:\/\//, "");
      const category = categorizeExtension(cleanPath);

      return {
        path: cleanPath,
        category,
        size: Number(file.size),
        offset: Number(file.offset),
        encrypted: (file.flags & 1) !== 0,
        metadata: {
          originalPath: file.path,
          md5: file.md5,
          flags: file.flags
        }
      };
    });
  }

  /**
   * Extract files from PCK to target directory
   */
  static async extractFiles(
    pckFilePath: string,
    outputDir: string,
    filesToExtract?: PckFileEntry[]
  ): Promise<{ extractedCount: number; errors: Array<{ file: string; error: string }> }> {
    const manifest = await this.readManifest(pckFilePath);
    if (!manifest) {
      throw new Error(`Failed to parse Godot PCK header in: ${pckFilePath}`);
    }

    const targetList = filesToExtract ?? manifest.files;
    let fileHandle: fs.FileHandle | undefined;
    const errors: Array<{ file: string; error: string }> = [];
    let extractedCount = 0;

    try {
      fileHandle = await fs.open(pckFilePath, "r");

      for (const entry of targetList) {
        try {
          const cleanRelPath = entry.path.replace(/^res:\/\//, "").replace(/\.\./g, "_");
          const outFilePath = path.join(outputDir, cleanRelPath);
          await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(outFilePath), { recursive: true });

          const size = Number(entry.size);
          const buf = Buffer.alloc(size);
          if (size > 0) {
            await fileHandle.read(buf, 0, size, Number(entry.offset));
          }
          await fs.writeFile(outFilePath, buf);
          extractedCount++;
        } catch (err: unknown) {
          errors.push({
            file: entry.path,
            error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)
          });
        }
      }

      return { extractedCount, errors };
    } finally {
      if (fileHandle) {
        await fileHandle.close();
      }
    }
  }
}

```

`src/parsers/pe/pe-resource-parser.ts`:

```ts
import * as fs from "node:fs/promises";
import * as path from "node:path";

export interface PeSection {
  name: string;
  virtualSize: number;
  virtualAddress: number;
  sizeOfRawData: number;
  pointerToRawData: number;
}

export interface PeResourceEntry {
  typeId: number;
  typeName: string;
  nameOrId: string | number;
  languageId: number;
  offset: number; // File offset
  size: number;
  dataRva: number;
}

export interface PeInfo {
  arch: "x86" | "x64" | "arm64" | "unknown";
  isDotNet: boolean;
  subsystem: number;
  sections: PeSection[];
  resources: PeResourceEntry[];
  framework: "Delphi" | ".NET" | "Electron" | "Qt" | "Native C/C++" | "Unknown";
}

const RESOURCE_TYPE_NAMES: Record<number, string> = {
  1: "RT_CURSOR",
  2: "RT_BITMAP",
  3: "RT_ICON",
  4: "RT_MENU",
  5: "RT_DIALOG",
  6: "RT_STRING",
  7: "RT_FONTDIR",
  8: "RT_FONT",
  9: "RT_ACCELERATOR",
  10: "RT_RCDATA",
  11: "RT_MESSAGETABLE",
  12: "RT_GROUP_CURSOR",
  14: "RT_GROUP_ICON",
  16: "RT_VERSION",
  24: "RT_MANIFEST"
};

export class PeResourceParser {
  /**
   * Parse PE headers, sections, and resource directory tree
   */
  static async parse(filePath: string): Promise<PeInfo | null> {
    let fileHandle: fs.FileHandle | undefined;
    try {
      fileHandle = await fs.open(filePath, "r");
      const stat = await fileHandle.stat();
      if (stat.size < 512) return null;

      // Read DOS header
      const dosBuf = Buffer.alloc(64);
      await fileHandle.read(dosBuf, 0, 64, 0);
      if (dosBuf.readUInt16LE(0) !== 0x5a4d) return null; // "MZ"

      const peOffset = dosBuf.readInt32LE(0x3c);
      if (peOffset + 256 > stat.size) return null;

      // Read PE header + optional header
      const peBuf = Buffer.alloc(512);
      await fileHandle.read(peBuf, 0, 512, peOffset);

      if (peBuf.readUInt32LE(0) !== 0x00004550) return null; // "PE\0\0"

      const machine = peBuf.readUInt16LE(4);
      const numberOfSections = peBuf.readUInt16LE(6);
      const sizeOfOptionalHeader = peBuf.readUInt16LE(20);

      const arch: PeInfo["arch"] =
        machine === 0x8664 ? "x64" : machine === 0x014c ? "x86" : machine === 0xaa64 ? "arm64" : "unknown";

      const optMagic = peBuf.readUInt16LE(24);
      const isPE32Plus = optMagic === 0x020b;

      // Data directories start at 96 for PE32, 112 for PE32+ (relative to optional header start at offset 24)
      const dataDirsOffset = 24 + (isPE32Plus ? 112 : 96);
      let resourceRva = 0;
      let resourceSize = 0;
      let clrRva = 0;

      if (dataDirsOffset + 16 * 8 <= peBuf.length) {
        // Resource Directory is index 2
        resourceRva = peBuf.readUInt32LE(dataDirsOffset + 2 * 8);
        resourceSize = peBuf.readUInt32LE(dataDirsOffset + 2 * 8 + 4);

        // CLR Header is index 14
        clrRva = peBuf.readUInt32LE(dataDirsOffset + 14 * 8);
      }

      const isDotNet = clrRva > 0;

      // Read section headers
      const sectionHeadersStart = peOffset + 24 + sizeOfOptionalHeader;
      const sectionsBuf = Buffer.alloc(numberOfSections * 40);
      await fileHandle.read(sectionsBuf, 0, sectionsBuf.length, sectionHeadersStart);

      const sections: PeSection[] = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < numberOfSections; i++) {
        const sOff = i * 40;
        const nameRaw = sectionsBuf.subarray(sOff, sOff + 8);
        const nullIdx = nameRaw.indexOf(0);
        const name = (nullIdx >= 0 ? nameRaw.subarray(0, nullIdx) : nameRaw).toString("ascii");

        const virtualSize = sectionsBuf.readUInt32LE(sOff + 8);
        const virtualAddress = sectionsBuf.readUInt32LE(sOff + 12);
        const sizeOfRawData = sectionsBuf.readUInt32LE(sOff + 16);
        const pointerToRawData = sectionsBuf.readUInt32LE(sOff + 20);

        sections.push({
          name,
          virtualSize,
          virtualAddress,
          sizeOfRawData,
          pointerToRawData
        });
      }

      // Helper to map RVA to file offset
      const rvaToFileOffset = (rva: number): number | null => {
        for (const s of sections) {
          const sEnd = s.virtualAddress + Math.max(s.virtualSize, s.sizeOfRawData);
          if (rva >= s.virtualAddress && rva < sEnd) {
            return s.pointerToRawData + (rva - s.virtualAddress);
          }
        }
        return null;
      };

      const resources: PeResourceEntry[] = [];

      // Parse resource tree if resource table exists
      if (resourceRva > 0 && resourceSize > 0) {
        const rootOffset = rvaToFileOffset(resourceRva);
        if (rootOffset !== null && rootOffset + resourceSize <= stat.size) {
          // Read entire resource section
          const rsrcBuf = Buffer.alloc(resourceSize);
          await fileHandle.read(rsrcBuf, 0, resourceSize, rootOffset);

          // Level 1: Resource Types
          this.parseResourceDir(rsrcBuf, 0, (typeId, nameOrId, langId, dataRva, dataSize) => {
            const dataFileOffset = rvaToFileOffset(dataRva);
            if (dataFileOffset !== null) {
              resources.push({
                typeId: typeof typeId === "number" ? typeId : -1,
                typeName: typeof typeId === "number" ? RESOURCE_TYPE_NAMES[typeId] || `TYPE_${typeId}` : String(typeId),
                nameOrId,
                languageId: langId,
                offset: dataFileOffset,
                size: dataSize,
                dataRva
              });
            }
          });
        }
      }

      // Detect Framework
      let framework: PeInfo["framework"] = "Native C/C++";
      if (isDotNet) {
        framework = ".NET";
      } else {
        // Sample strings in binary for Delphi / Qt / Electron
        const sampleSize = Math.min(Number(stat.size), 8 * 1024 * 1024);
        const sampleBuf = Buffer.alloc(sampleSize);
        await fileHandle.read(sampleBuf, 0, sampleSize, 0);
        const sampleStr = sampleBuf.toString("latin1");

        if (sampleStr.includes("Borland") || sampleStr.includes("Delphi") || sampleStr.includes("TPF0")) {
          framework = "Delphi";
        } else if (sampleStr.includes("electron.app") || sampleStr.includes("v8_context_snapshot.bin")) {
          framework = "Electron";
        } else if (sampleStr.includes("Qt5Core.dll") || sampleStr.includes("Qt6Core.dll") || sampleStr.includes("QtCore")) {
          framework = "Qt";
        }
      }

      return {
        arch,
        isDotNet,
        subsystem: peBuf.readUInt16LE(24 + (isPE32Plus ? 68 : 68)),
        sections,
        resources,
        framework
      };
    } catch {
      return null;
    } finally {
      if (fileHandle) {
        await fileHandle.close();
      }
    }
  }

  /**
   * Traverse the 3-level PE resource directory tree
   */
  private static parseResourceDir(
    rsrc: Buffer,
    dirOffset: number,
    onLeaf: (typeId: number | string, nameOrId: number | string, langId: number, dataRva: number, dataSize: number) => void
  ): void {
    if (dirOffset + 16 > rsrc.length) return;

    const numNamed = rsrc.readUInt16LE(dirOffset + 12);
    const numId = rsrc.readUInt16LE(dirOffset + 14);
    const totalEntries = numNamed + numId;

    let entryOffset = dirOffset + 16;

    // Level 1: Types
    for (let i = 0; i < totalEntries; i++) {
      if (entryOffset + 8 > rsrc.length) break;

      const typeIdRaw = rsrc.readUInt32LE(entryOffset);
      const subOffsetRaw = rsrc.readUInt32LE(entryOffset + 4);
      entryOffset += 8;

      let typeVal: number | string = typeIdRaw;
      if ((typeIdRaw & 0x80000000) !== 0) {
        typeVal = this.readUnicodeString(rsrc, typeIdRaw & 0x7fffffff);
      }

      if ((subOffsetRaw & 0x80000000) !== 0) {
        const level2Offset = subOffsetRaw & 0x7fffffff;
        if (level2Offset + 16 > rsrc.length) continue;

        const l2NumNamed = rsrc.readUInt16LE(level2Offset + 12);
        const l2NumId = rsrc.readUInt16LE(level2Offset + 14);
        const l2Total = l2NumNamed + l2NumId;
        let l2EntryOffset = level2Offset + 16;

        // Level 2: Names / IDs
        for (let j = 0; j < l2Total; j++) {
          if (l2EntryOffset + 8 > rsrc.length) break;

          const nameIdRaw = rsrc.readUInt32LE(l2EntryOffset);
          const l2SubOffsetRaw = rsrc.readUInt32LE(l2EntryOffset + 4);
          l2EntryOffset += 8;

          let nameVal: number | string = nameIdRaw;
          if ((nameIdRaw & 0x80000000) !== 0) {
            nameVal = this.readUnicodeString(rsrc, nameIdRaw & 0x7fffffff);
          }

          if ((l2SubOffsetRaw & 0x80000000) !== 0) {
            const level3Offset = l2SubOffsetRaw & 0x7fffffff;
            if (level3Offset + 16 > rsrc.length) continue;

            const l3Total = rsrc.readUInt16LE(level3Offset + 12) + rsrc.readUInt16LE(level3Offset + 14);
            let l3EntryOffset = level3Offset + 16;

            // Level 3: Languages
            for (let k = 0; k < l3Total; k++) {
              if (l3EntryOffset + 8 > rsrc.length) break;

              const langId = rsrc.readUInt32LE(l3EntryOffset);
              const dataEntryOffset = rsrc.readUInt32LE(l3EntryOffset + 4);
              l3EntryOffset += 8;

              // Data entry leaf
              if ((dataEntryOffset & 0x80000000) === 0 && dataEntryOffset + 16 <= rsrc.length) {
                const dataRva = rsrc.readUInt32LE(dataEntryOffset);
                const dataSize = rsrc.readUInt32LE(dataEntryOffset + 4);
                onLeaf(typeVal, nameVal, langId, dataRva, dataSize);
              }
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  private static readUnicodeString(rsrc: Buffer, strOffset: number): string {
    if (strOffset + 2 > rsrc.length) return "UNKNOWN";
    const len = rsrc.readUInt16LE(strOffset);
    if (strOffset + 2 + len * 2 > rsrc.length) return "UNKNOWN";
    return rsrc.subarray(strOffset + 2, strOffset + 2 + len * 2).toString("utf16le");
  }

  /**
   * Reconstruct standalone Windows .ico files from RT_GROUP_ICON and RT_ICON resources
   */
  static async extractIcons(
    filePath: string,
    resources: PeResourceEntry[]
  ): Promise<Array<{ name: string; buffer: Buffer }>> {
    const groupIcons = resources.filter((r) => r.typeId === 14); // RT_GROUP_ICON
    const singleIcons = new Map<number, PeResourceEntry>();

    for (const r of resources) {
      if (r.typeId === 3 && typeof r.nameOrId === "number") {
        singleIcons.set(r.nameOrId, r);
      }
    }

    if (groupIcons.length === 0) return [];

    let fileHandle: fs.FileHandle | undefined;
    const icons: Array<{ name: string; buffer: Buffer }> = [];

    try {
      fileHandle = await fs.open(filePath, "r");

      for (const grp of groupIcons) {
        const grpBuf = Buffer.alloc(grp.size);
        await fileHandle.read(grpBuf, 0, grp.size, grp.offset);

        if (grpBuf.length < 6) continue;
        const count = grpBuf.readUInt16LE(4);

        if (grpBuf.length < 6 + count * 14) continue;

        // Collect all icon images for this group
        const imageBuffers: Buffer[] = [];
        const dirEntries: Buffer[] = [];
        let runningOffset = 6 + count * 16; // ICONDIR (6) + N * ICONDIRENTRY (16)

        for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
          const entryOff = 6 + i * 14;
          const iconId = grpBuf.readUInt16LE(entryOff + 12);
          const iconRes = singleIcons.get(iconId);

          if (!iconRes) continue;

          const imgBuf = Buffer.alloc(iconRes.size);
          await fileHandle.read(imgBuf, 0, iconRes.size, iconRes.offset);

          // Build 16-byte ICONDIRENTRY
          const dirEntry = Buffer.alloc(16);
          // Copy width, height, colorCount, reserved, planes, bitCount, bytesInRes
          grpBuf.copy(dirEntry, 0, entryOff, entryOff + 12);
          dirEntry.writeUInt32LE(runningOffset, 12); // Image data offset

          dirEntries.push(dirEntry);
          imageBuffers.push(imgBuf);
          runningOffset += imgBuf.length;
        }

        if (imageBuffers.length > 0) {
          const header = Buffer.alloc(6);
          header.writeUInt16LE(0, 0); // reserved
          header.writeUInt16LE(1, 2); // type 1 = icon
          header.writeUInt16LE(dirEntries.length, 4);

          const fullIco = Buffer.concat([header, ...dirEntries, ...imageBuffers]);
          icons.push({
            name: `icon_${grp.nameOrId}.ico`,
            buffer: fullIco
          });
        }
      }

      return icons;
    } finally {
      if (fileHandle) {
        await fileHandle.close();
      }
    }
  }
}

```

`src/parsers/unity/bundle-parser.ts`:

```ts
import * as fs from "node:fs/promises";

export interface UnityBundleInfo {
  signature: "UnityFS" | "UnityWeb" | "UnityRaw" | "SerializedFile";
  formatVersion: number;
  engineVersion: string;
  minPlayerVersion?: string;
  size: bigint;
  compressedSize?: number;
  uncompressedSize?: number;
}

export class UnityBundleParser {
  /**
   * Check if file is a Unity bundle or serialized asset file
   */
  static async readBundleInfo(filePath: string): Promise<UnityBundleInfo | null> {
    let fileHandle: fs.FileHandle | undefined;
    try {
      fileHandle = await fs.open(filePath, "r");
      const stat = await fileHandle.stat();
      if (stat.size < 32) return null;

      const headerBuf = Buffer.alloc(256);
      await fileHandle.read(headerBuf, 0, 256, 0);

      // Check for string signature at start
      const signatureStr = headerBuf.subarray(0, 16).toString("ascii").replace(/\0.*$/, "").trim();

      if (signatureStr === "UnityFS" || signatureStr === "UnityWeb" || signatureStr === "UnityRaw") {
        // Read format version
        const sigLen = signatureStr.length + 1; // null-terminated
        const formatVersion = headerBuf.readUInt32BE(sigLen);

        // Unity version string null-terminated
        const unityVerStart = sigLen + 4;
        const nullIdx = headerBuf.indexOf(0, unityVerStart);
        const engineVersion = nullIdx > unityVerStart
          ? headerBuf.subarray(unityVerStart, nullIdx).toString("ascii")
          : "Unknown";

        return {
          signature: signatureStr,
          formatVersion,
          engineVersion,
          size: BigInt(stat.size)
        };
      }

      // Check SerializedFile (usually starts with metadataSize, fileSize in Big-Endian)
      // SerializedFile header has version at offset 8 (or 12 in 64-bit format)
      // Usually version is between 9 and 22 for Unity 4 to Unity 2022/6
      const possibleVer = headerBuf.readUInt32BE(8);
      if (possibleVer >= 9 && possibleVer <= 30) {
        return {
          signature: "SerializedFile",
          formatVersion: possibleVer,
          engineVersion: `Unity Serialized v${possibleVer}`,
          size: BigInt(stat.size)
        };
      }

      return null;
    } catch {
      return null;
    } finally {
      if (fileHandle) {
        await fileHandle.close();
      }
    }
  }
}
```
