`src/parsers/unreal/pak-parser.ts`:

```ts
import * as fs from "node:fs/promises";

export const UE_PAK_MAGIC = 0x5a6f12e1; // 0xE1, 0x12, 0x6F, 0x5A

export interface UnrealPakInfo {
  magic: number;
  version: number;
  indexOffset: bigint;
  indexSize: bigint;
  isEncrypted: boolean;
  compressionMethods?: string[];
  footerOffset: number;
}

export class UnrealPakParser {
  /**
   * Search for FPakInfo in the last 512 bytes of a file
   */
  static async readPakInfo(filePath: string): Promise<UnrealPakInfo | null> {
    let fileHandle: fs.FileHandle | undefined;
    try {
      fileHandle = await fs.open(filePath, "r");
      const stat = await fileHandle.stat();
      if (stat.size < 64) return null;

      const footerReadSize = Math.min(512, Number(stat.size));
      const footerBuf = Buffer.alloc(footerReadSize);
      await fileHandle.read(footerBuf, 0, footerReadSize, stat.size - footerReadSize);

      // Search for UE_PAK_MAGIC
      for (let offset = footerReadSize - 4; offset >= 0; offset--) {
        if (footerBuf.readUInt32LE(offset) === UE_PAK_MAGIC) {
          // Version is 4 bytes after magic (or earlier depending on format version)
          // In UE Pak format:
          // Version 1..7: Magic is at offset 0 of FPakInfo, or at the end.
          // In standard UE4 FPakInfo (v8+):
          // Format has:
          // ...
          // Magic: uint32
          // Version: int32
          // IndexOffset: int64
          // IndexSize: int64
          // IndexSHA1: 20 bytes
          // bEncrypted: uint8
          // Let's parse with safe guards
          const version = offset + 8 <= footerReadSize ? footerBuf.readInt32LE(offset + 4) : 0;
          let indexOffset = 0n;
          let indexSize = 0n;
          let isEncrypted = false;

          if (offset >= 16) {
            // In modern UE4/5 (v8A-v11), FPakInfo has index offset and size preceding or following magic
            // Let's check common layout
            if (offset + 24 <= footerReadSize) {
              indexOffset = footerBuf.readBigInt64LE(offset + 8);
              indexSize = footerBuf.readBigInt64LE(offset + 16);
            }
          }

          // Check encrypted flag (last byte or near end)
          if (offset + 45 <= footerReadSize) {
            isEncrypted = footerBuf.readUInt8(offset + 44) !== 0;
          }

          return {
            magic: UE_PAK_MAGIC,
            version: version > 0 && version < 20 ? version : 8,
            indexOffset,
            indexSize,
            isEncrypted,
            footerOffset: Number(stat.size) - (footerReadSize - offset)
          };
        }
      }

      return null;
    } catch {
      return null;
    } finally {
      if (fileHandle) {
        await fileHandle.close();
      }
    }
  }
}

```

`src/rea/binary-scanner.ts`:

```ts
import * as fs from "node:fs/promises";
import * as path from "node:path";
import { parsePeHeaderArch, readHeaderBytes } from "../utils/file-utils.js";
import type { BinaryScanResult } from "../types/index.js";

// Rijndael AES SubBytes first 16 bytes
const AES_SBOX_PREFIX = Buffer.from([
  0x63, 0x7c, 0x77, 0x7b, 0xf2, 0x6b, 0x6f, 0xc5,
  0x30, 0x01, 0x67, 0x2b, 0xfe, 0xd7, 0xab, 0x76
]);

// IL2CPP global-metadata.dat magic: 0xFAB11BAF (in little endian: AF 1B B1 FA)
const IL2CPP_METADATA_MAGIC = 0xfab11baf;

export class BinaryScanner {
  /**
   * Calculate Shannon entropy of a byte buffer
   */
  static calculateEntropy(buffer: Buffer): number {
    if (buffer.length === 0) return 0;
    const frequencies = new Array(256).fill(0);
    for (let i = 0; i < buffer.length; i++) {
      frequencies[buffer[i]]++;
    }

    let entropy = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < 256; i++) {
      if (frequencies[i] > 0) {
        const p = frequencies[i] / buffer.length;
        entropy -= p * Math.log2(p);
      }
    }
    return entropy;
  }

  /**
   * Search for hex string AES keys (e.g. 0x[64 hex chars] or 64 hex chars)
   */
  static searchHexAesStrings(buffer: Buffer): Array<{ keyHex: string; offset: number }> {
    const text = buffer.toString("latin1");
    const regex = /(?:0x)?([A-Fa-f0-9]{64})/g;
    const results: Array<{ keyHex: string; offset: number }> = [];

    let match: RegExpExecArray | null;
    while ((match = regex.exec(text)) !== null) {
      const keyHex = match[1].toLowerCase();
      // Filter out trivial repeating patterns (e.g. 000000... or ffffff...)
      if (!/^(.)\1+$/.test(keyHex)) {
        results.push({
          keyHex: `0x${keyHex}`,
          offset: match.index
        });
      }
    }

    return results;
  }

  /**
   * Search for binary AES keys near AES S-box constants
   */
  static searchNearSBox(buffer: Buffer): Array<{ keyHex: string; offset: number; entropy: number }> {
    const candidates: Array<{ keyHex: string; offset: number; entropy: number }> = [];
    let sboxOffset = -1;

    // Search for S-box prefix
    for (let i = 0; i <= buffer.length - AES_SBOX_PREFIX.length; i++) {
      if (buffer.subarray(i, i + AES_SBOX_PREFIX.length).equals(AES_SBOX_PREFIX)) {
        sboxOffset = i;
        break;
      }
    }

    if (sboxOffset >= 0) {
      // Look within +/- 8KB of S-box for 32-byte high-entropy buffers
      const searchStart = Math.max(0, sboxOffset - 8192);
      const searchEnd = Math.min(buffer.length - 32, sboxOffset + 8192);

      for (let pos = searchStart; pos <= searchEnd; pos += 4) {
        const chunk = buffer.subarray(pos, pos + 32);
        const entropy = this.calculateEntropy(chunk);
        // AES key entropy is typically > 4.5
        if (entropy > 4.6) {
          candidates.push({
            keyHex: `0x${chunk.toString("hex")}`,
            offset: pos,
            entropy
          });
        }
      }
    }

    return candidates;
  }

  /**
   * Inspect IL2CPP global-metadata.dat
   */
  static async inspectIl2CppMetadata(filePath: string): Promise<{ version: number; validMagic: boolean } | null> {
    try {
      const header = await readHeaderBytes(filePath, 32);
      if (header.length < 8) return null;

      const magic = header.readUInt32LE(0);
      if (magic === IL2CPP_METADATA_MAGIC) {
        const version = header.readInt32LE(4);
        return { version, validMagic: true };
      }
      return null;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Perform comprehensive binary scan on an executable or dump
   */
  static async scanBinary(binaryPath: string): Promise<BinaryScanResult> {
    const arch = await parsePeHeaderArch(binaryPath);
    const stat = await fs.stat(binaryPath);
    const readSize = Math.min(Number(stat.size), 64 * 1024 * 1024); // Scan up to 64MB

    let fileHandle: fs.FileHandle | undefined;
    const candidateAesKeys: BinaryScanResult["candidateAesKeys"] = [];
    const stringsFound: string[] = [];
    const recommendations: string[] = [];

    try {
      fileHandle = await fs.open(binaryPath, "r");
      const buffer = Buffer.alloc(readSize);
      await fileHandle.read(buffer, 0, readSize, 0);

      // 1. Search hex string AES keys
      const hexKeys = this.searchHexAesStrings(buffer);
      for (const hk of hexKeys) {
        const rawBuf = Buffer.from(hk.keyHex.replace("0x", ""), "hex");
        const entropy = this.calculateEntropy(rawBuf);
        candidateAesKeys.push({
          keyHex: hk.keyHex,
          offset: hk.offset,
          entropy,
          confidence: entropy > 4.2 ? "high" : "medium",
          pattern: "Hex String Pattern"
        });
      }

      // 2. Search binary S-Box proximity keys
      const sboxKeys = this.searchNearSBox(buffer);
      for (const sk of sboxKeys.slice(0, 5)) {
        candidateAesKeys.push({
          keyHex: sk.keyHex,
          offset: sk.offset,
          entropy: sk.entropy,
          confidence: "medium",
          pattern: "Near Rijndael S-Box"
        });
      }

      // Check for common engine strings
      const asciiText = buffer.toString("latin1");
      if (asciiText.includes("UnrealEngine")) stringsFound.push("UnrealEngine signature");
      if (asciiText.includes("FArchive")) stringsFound.push("Unreal Serialization (FArchive)");
      if (asciiText.includes("UnityPlayer")) stringsFound.push("UnityPlayer runtime reference");
      if (asciiText.includes("il2cpp")) stringsFound.push("IL2CPP runtime symbols");
      if (asciiText.includes("Godot Engine")) stringsFound.push("Godot Engine banner string");

      // Check sibling files for global-metadata.dat
      let il2cppMetadata: BinaryScanResult["il2cppMetadata"];
      const parentDir = path.dirname(binaryPath);
      const possibleMetadataPaths = [
        path.join(parentDir, "il2cpp_data", "Metadata", "global-metadata.dat"),
        path.join(parentDir, "Metadata", "global-metadata.dat"),
        path.join(parentDir, "global-metadata.dat")
      ];

      for (const metaPath of possibleMetadataPaths) {
        try {
          const metaStat = await fs.stat(metaPath);
          if (metaStat.isFile()) {
            const res = await this.inspectIl2CppMetadata(metaPath);
            if (res) {
              il2cppMetadata = {
                validMagic: res.validMagic,
                version: res.version,
                offset: 0
              };
              recommendations.push(`Found IL2CPP global-metadata.dat v${res.version}. Reconstruct CIL bytecode with Cpp2IL (SamboyCoding/Cpp2IL) or dump signatures with Il2CppDumper / REA.`);
              break;
            }
          }
        } catch {
          // Continue
        }
      }

      if (candidateAesKeys.length > 0) {
        recommendations.push(`Detected ${candidateAesKeys.length} candidate AES-256 keys. Test them with 'ripper extract <pak> --aes-key <key>'`);
      } else {
        recommendations.push("No obvious plaintext AES key found. Consider using Ghidra (static decompilation) or Frida (dynamic runtime hooking of FAES::Decrypt).");
      }

      return {
        binaryPath,
        arch,
        candidateAesKeys: candidateAesKeys.slice(0, 10),
        il2cppMetadata,
        stringsFound,
        recommendations
      };
    } finally {
      if (fileHandle) {
        await fileHandle.close();
      }
    }
  }
}

```

`src/rea/rea-bridge.ts`:

```ts
import * as path from "node:path";
import { exec } from "node:child_process";
import { promisify } from "node:util";
import { BinaryScanner } from "./binary-scanner.js";
import type { BinaryScanResult } from "../types/index.js";

const execAsync = promisify(exec);

export interface ReaEvidenceBundle {
  targetBinary: string;
  architecture: string;
  evidenceType: "GameBinaryAnalysis";
  timestamp: string;
  findings: {
    candidateAesKeys: Array<{ key: string; offset: number; confidence: string }>;
    il2cppVersion?: number;
    signatures: string[];
  };
  reaTaskPrompt: string;
}

export class ReaBridge {
  /**
   * Check if REA CLI is installed in environment
   */
  static async isReaAvailable(): Promise<boolean> {
    try {
      await execAsync("rea --version");
      return true;
    } catch {
      return false;
    }
  }

  /**
   * Run binary inspection and generate REA evidence bundle
   */
  static async inspectBinary(binaryPath: string): Promise<{
    scan: BinaryScanResult;
    bundle: ReaEvidenceBundle;
  }> {
    const scan = await BinaryScanner.scanBinary(binaryPath);

    const reaPrompt = [
      `Target: ${path.basename(binaryPath)} (${scan.arch})`,
      scan.candidateAesKeys.length > 0
        ? `Candidate AES keys detected: ${scan.candidateAesKeys.map((k) => k.keyHex).join(", ")}`
        : "No direct AES keys in plaintext; trace FAES::Decrypt via Ghidra or hook dynamically with Frida.",
      scan.il2cppMetadata
        ? `IL2CPP metadata detected (version ${scan.il2cppMetadata.version}). Reconstruct CIL bytecode with Cpp2IL or trace il2cpp_init.`
        : "Standard native binary.",
      "Objective: Confirm decryption keys / recovery offsets."
    ].join("\n");

    const bundle: ReaEvidenceBundle = {
      targetBinary: binaryPath,
      architecture: scan.arch,
      evidenceType: "GameBinaryAnalysis",
      timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),
      findings: {
        candidateAesKeys: scan.candidateAesKeys.map((k) => ({
          key: k.keyHex,
          offset: k.offset,
          confidence: k.confidence
        })),
        il2cppVersion: scan.il2cppMetadata?.version,
        signatures: scan.stringsFound
      },
      reaTaskPrompt: reaPrompt
    };

    return { scan, bundle };
  }

  /**
   * Execute REA CLI task if available
   */
  static async runReaTask(binaryPath: string, taskDescription?: string): Promise<{ success: boolean; output: string }> {
    const isAvail = await this.isReaAvailable();
    if (!isAvail) {
      return {
        success: false,
        output: "REA CLI ('rea') is not found in PATH. Install morluto/rea via 'npm install -g @morluto/rea' or run REA as an MCP server."
      };
    }

    try {
      const task = taskDescription ?? "Extract AES encryption keys and analyze binary offsets";
      const cmd = `rea inspect "${binaryPath}" --task "${task}"`;
      const { stdout, stderr } = await execAsync(cmd);
      return {
        success: true,
        output: stdout || stderr
      };
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      return {
        success: false,
        output: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)
      };
    }
  }
}
```

```ts
export type EngineType = "unity" | "unreal" | "godot" | "pe-software" | "electron" | "unknown";

export type AssetCategory =
  | "texture"
  | "mesh"
  | "audio"
  | "scene"
  | "script"
  | "shader"
  | "font"
  | "video"
  | "animation"
  | "config"
  | "other";

export interface EngineDetails {
  framework?: string;
  isIL2CPP?: boolean;
  isMono?: boolean;
  encrypted?: boolean;
  entryPoint?: string;
  markersFound: string[];
  detectedFiles: string[];
  notes?: string[];
}

export interface EngineDetectionResult {
  engine: EngineType;
  confidence: number; // 0.0 to 1.0
  version?: string;
  arch?: "x86" | "x64" | "arm64" | "unknown";
  containerType?: string;
  targetPath: string;
  details: EngineDetails;
}

export interface AssetItem {

  category: AssetCategory;
  size: number;
  offset?: number;
  format?: string;

  totalBytes: number;
}
```

```ts
  ".avi": "video",

  // Animations
  ".anim": "animation",
  ".controller": "animation",

  // Config & Metadata
  ".json": "config",
  ".xml": "config",
  ".yaml": "config",
  ".yml": "config",
  ".ini": "config",
  ".cfg": "config",
  ".tres": "config", // Godot resource
  ".res": "config"

export function categorizeExtension(filePath: string): AssetCategory {
  const ext = path.extname(filePath).toLowerCase();
  return EXTENSION_CATEGORIES[ext] ?? "other";
}

export async function readHeaderBytes(
  filePath: string,
  byteCount = 1024
): Promise<Buffer> {
  let fileHandle: fs.FileHandle | undefined;
  try {
    fileHandle = await fs.open(filePath, "r");
    const stat = await fileHandle.stat();
    const length = Math.min(byteCount, stat.size);
    const buffer = Buffer.alloc(length);
    if (length > 0) {
      await fileHandle.read(buffer, 0, length, 0);
    }
    return buffer;
  } finally {
    if (fileHandle) {
      await fileHandle.close();
    }
  }
}

export async function readTailBytes(
  filePath: string,
  byteCount = 1024
): Promise<Buffer> {
  let fileHandle: fs.FileHandle | undefined;
  try {
    fileHandle = await fs.open(filePath, "r");
    const stat = await fileHandle.stat();
    const length = Math.min(byteCount, stat.size);
    const buffer = Buffer.alloc(length);
    if (length > 0) {
      const offset = stat.size - length;
      await fileHandle.read(buffer, 0, length, offset);
    }
    return buffer;
  } finally {
    if (fileHandle) {
      await fileHandle.close();
    }
  }
}

export async function listFilesRecursively(
  dir: string,
  maxDepth = 5,
  currentDepth = 0
): Promise<string[]> {
  if (currentDepth > maxDepth) return [];
  const entries = await fs.readdir(dir, { withFileTypes: true });
  const files: string[] = [];

  for (const entry of entries) {
    const fullPath = path.join(dir, entry.name);
    if (entry.isDirectory()) {
      const subFiles = await listFilesRecursively(
        fullPath,
        maxDepth,
        currentDepth + 1
      );
      files.push(...subFiles);
    } else if (entry.isFile()) {
      files.push(fullPath);
    }
  }

  return files;
}

export async function parsePeHeaderArch(
  filePath: string
): Promise<"x86" | "x64" | "arm64" | "unknown"> {
  try {
    const header = await readHeaderBytes(filePath, 4096);
    // Check MZ signature
    if (header.length < 64 || header.readUInt16LE(0) !== 0x5a4d) {
      return "unknown";
    }
    // e_lfanew at 0x3C
    const peOffset = header.readInt32LE(0x3c);
    if (peOffset + 6 > header.length) {
      return "unknown";
    }
    // Check 'PE\0\0' signature (0x00004550)
    if (header.readUInt32LE(peOffset) !== 0x00004550) {
      return "unknown";
    }
    // Machine field
    const machine = header.readUInt16LE(peOffset + 4);
    switch (machine) {
      case 0x014c:
        return "x86";
      case 0x8664:
        return "x64";
      case 0xaa64:
        return "arm64";
      default:
        return "unknown";
    }
  } catch {
    return "unknown";
  }
}
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach } from "vitest";
import * as fs from "node:fs/promises";
import * as path from "node:path";
import * as os from "node:os";
import { AdapterRegistry } from "../src/adapters/index.js";
import { GDPC_MAGIC } from "../src/parsers/godot/pck-parser.js";

describe("AdapterRegistry", () => {
  let tmpDir: string;

  beforeEach(async () => {
    tmpDir = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "registry-test-"));
  });

  afterEach(async () => {
    await fs.rm(tmpDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  });

  it("should get registered adapters by engine type", () => {
    const godot = AdapterRegistry.getAdapter("godot");
    const unreal = AdapterRegistry.getAdapter("unreal");
    const unity = AdapterRegistry.getAdapter("unity");

    expect(godot?.engine).toBe("godot");
    expect(unreal?.engine).toBe("unreal");
    expect(unity?.engine).toBe("unity");
  });

  it("should resolve adapter automatically for Godot target", async () => {
    const pckPath = path.join(tmpDir, "game.pck");
    const pckHeader = Buffer.alloc(32);
    pckHeader.writeUInt32LE(GDPC_MAGIC, 0);
    pckHeader.writeUInt32LE(1, 4);
    pckHeader.writeUInt32LE(4, 8);
    pckHeader.writeUInt32LE(1, 12);
    pckHeader.writeUInt32LE(0, 16);
    await fs.writeFile(pckPath, pckHeader);

    const { adapter, engine } = await AdapterRegistry.resolveAdapter(pckPath);
    expect(engine).toBe("godot");
    expect(adapter.engine).toBe("godot");
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach } from "vitest";
import * as fs from "node:fs/promises";
import * as path from "node:path";
import * as os from "node:os";
import { exec } from "node:child_process";
import { promisify } from "node:util";
import { GDPC_MAGIC } from "../src/parsers/godot/pck-parser.js";

const execAsync = promisify(exec);

describe("CLI commands", () => {
  let tmpDir: string;
  let cliPath: string;

  beforeEach(async () => {
    tmpDir = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "cli-test-"));
    cliPath = path.resolve("./dist/cli/index.js");
  });

  afterEach(async () => {
    await fs.rm(tmpDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  });

  it("should execute 'ripper detect' successfully", async () => {
    const pckPath = path.join(tmpDir, "test.pck");
    const pckHeader = Buffer.alloc(32);
    pckHeader.writeUInt32LE(GDPC_MAGIC, 0);
    pckHeader.writeUInt32LE(1, 4);
    pckHeader.writeUInt32LE(4, 3);
    pckHeader.writeUInt32LE(0, 12);
    pckHeader.writeUInt32LE(0, 16);
    await fs.writeFile(pckPath, pckHeader);

    const { stdout } = await execAsync(`node "${cliPath}" detect "${pckPath}"`);
    expect(stdout).toContain("GODOT");
    expect(stdout).toContain("Engine Detection Results");
  });

  it("should execute 'ripper rea-inspect' on binary with AES key", async () => {
    const binPath = path.join(tmpDir, "test.exe");
    const buf = Buffer.alloc(1024);
    buf.write("0x11223344556677889900aabbccddeeff11223344556677889900aabbccddeeff", 100, "ascii");
    await fs.writeFile(binPath, buf);

    const { stdout } = await execAsync(`node "${cliPath}" rea-inspect "${binPath}"`);
    expect(stdout).toContain("Candidate AES-256 Keys Discovered");
    expect(stdout).toContain("0x11223344556677889900aabbccddeeff11223344556677889900aabbccddeeff");
  });
});
```

`tests/detector.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach } from "vitest";
import * as fs from "node:fs/promises";
import * as path from "node:path";
import * as os from "node:os";
import { EngineDetector } from "../src/core/detector/engine-detector.js";
import { UE_PAK_MAGIC } from "../src/parsers/unreal/pak-parser.js";
import { GDPC_MAGIC } from "../src/parsers/godot/pck-parser.js";

describe("EngineDetector", () => {
  let tmpDir: string;

  beforeEach(async () => {
    tmpDir = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "detector-test-"));
  });

  afterEach(async () => {
    await fs.rm(tmpDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  });

  it("should detect Unity game directory with UnityPlayer.dll and *_Data", async () => {
    const unityDir = path.join(tmpDir, "MyUnityGame");
    const dataDir = path.join(unityDir, "MyUnityGame_Data");
    await fs.mkdir(dataDir, { recursive: true });
    await fs.writeFile(path.join(unityDir, "UnityPlayer.dll"), "FAKE_UNITY_DLL");
    await fs.writeFile(path.join(unityDir, "GameAssembly.dll"), "FAKE_IL2CPP_DLL");
    await fs.writeFile(path.join(dataDir, "globalgamemanagers"), "GLOBAL_MANAGERS");

    const result = await EngineDetector.detect(unityDir);
    expect(result.engine).toBe("unity");
    expect(result.confidence).toBeGreaterThan(0.8);
    expect(result.details.isIL2CPP).toBe(true);
    expect(result.details.markersFound).toContain("UnityPlayer.dll");
  });

  it("should detect Unreal Engine game directory with Content/Paks", async () => {
    const ueDir = path.join(tmpDir, "MyUnrealGame");
    const paksDir = path.join(ueDir, "Content", "Paks");
    await fs.mkdir(paksDir, { recursive: true });

    // Create a mock .pak file with FPakInfo footer
    const pakPath = path.join(paksDir, "PakChunk0.pak");
    const pakData = Buffer.alloc(1024);
    // Write UE_PAK_MAGIC near footer (e.g. at 1024 - 48)
    pakData.writeUInt32LE(UE_PAK_MAGIC, 1024 - 48);
    pakData.writeInt32LE(8, 1024 - 44); // version 8
    await fs.writeFile(pakPath, pakData);

    const result = await EngineDetector.detect(ueDir);
    expect(result.engine).toBe("unreal");
    expect(result.confidence).toBeGreaterThan(0.7);
    expect(result.details.markersFound.some((m) => m.includes("PakChunk0.pak"))).toBe(true);
  });

  it("should detect Godot game with standalone .pck file", async () => {
    const godotDir = path.join(tmpDir, "MyGodotGame");
    await fs.mkdir(godotDir, { recursive: true });

    const pckPath = path.join(godotDir, "game.pck");
    const pckHeader = Buffer.alloc(32);
    pckHeader.writeUInt32LE(GDPC_MAGIC, 0);
    pckHeader.writeUInt32LE(1, 4);
    pckHeader.writeUInt32LE(4, 8); // engine major 4
    pckHeader.writeUInt32LE(2, 12); // minor 2
    pckHeader.writeUInt32LE(0, 16); // patch 0
    await fs.writeFile(pckPath, pckHeader);

    const result = await EngineDetector.detect(godotDir);
    expect(result.engine).toBe("godot");
    expect(result.confidence).toBeGreaterThan(0.7);
    expect(result.version).toBe("4.2.0");
  });
});
```

`tests/godot-pck.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach } from "vitest";
import * as fs from "node:fs/promises";
import * as path from "node:path";
import * as os from "node:os";
import { GodotPckParser, GDPC_MAGIC } from "../src/parsers/godot/pck-parser.js";
import { GodotAdapter } from "../src/adapters/godot-adapter.js";

describe("GodotPckParser & GodotAdapter", () => {
  let tmpDir: string;
  let samplePckPath: string;

  beforeEach(async () => {
    tmpDir = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "godot-test-"));
    samplePckPath = path.join(tmpDir, "test_game.pck");

    // Build synthetic Godot PCK v1 binary:
    // Header:
    //   Magic: uint32 (GDPC = 0x43504447)
    //   Format: uint32 (1)
    //   Major: uint32 (3)
    //   Minor: uint32 (5)
    //   Patch: uint32 (2)
    //   16 uint32 reserved = 64 bytes (zeroed)
    //   File count: uint32 (2)
    // Entry 1: "res://icon.png"
    // Entry 2: "res://main.gd"

    const header = Buffer.alloc(20 + 64 + 4);
    header.writeUInt32LE(GDPC_MAGIC, 0);
    header.writeUInt32LE(1, 4); // format v1
    header.writeUInt32LE(3, 8); // engine major 3
    header.writeUInt32LE(5, 12); // minor 5
    header.writeUInt32LE(2, 16); // patch 2
    // 64 bytes reserved are 0
    header.writeUInt32LE(2, 20 + 64); // 2 files

    // Entry 1 metadata
    const path1 = Buffer.from("res://icon.png", "utf-8");
    const pathLen1 = Buffer.alloc(4);
    pathLen1.writeUInt32LE(path1.length);
    const meta1 = Buffer.alloc(32);
    const file1Data = Buffer.from("PNG_FAKE_IMAGE_DATA_12345");
    // Offset will be calculated after full index
    // Let's compute offsets:
    // Header size = 88 bytes.
    // Entry 1: 4 + 14 + 32 = 50 bytes.
    // Entry 2: "res://scripts/player.gd" (23 bytes): 4 + 23 + 32 = 59 bytes.
    // Total index size = 88 + 50 + 59 = 197 bytes.
    const file1Offset = 197n;
    const file1Size = BigInt(file1Data.length);
    meta1.writeBigInt64LE(file1Offset, 0);
    meta1.writeBigInt64LE(file1Size, 8);

    // Entry 2 metadata
    const path2 = Buffer.from("res://scripts/player.gd", "utf-8");
    const pathLen2 = Buffer.alloc(4);
    pathLen2.writeUInt32LE(path2.length);
    const meta2 = Buffer.alloc(32);
    const file2Data = Buffer.from("extends KinematicBody2D\nfunc _ready(): pass");
    const file2Offset = file1Offset + file1Size;
    const file2Size = BigInt(file2Data.length);
    meta2.writeBigInt64LE(file2Offset, 0);
    meta2.writeBigInt64LE(file2Size, 8);

    const fullPck = Buffer.concat([
      header,
      pathLen1,
      path1,
      meta1,
      pathLen2,
      path2,
      meta2,
      file1Data,
      file2Data
    ]);

    await fs.writeFile(samplePckPath, fullPck);
  });

  afterEach(async () => {
    await fs.rm(tmpDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  });

  it("should successfully parse synthetic Godot PCK header and files", async () => {
    const manifest = await GodotPckParser.readManifest(samplePckPath);
    expect(manifest).not.toBeNull();
    expect(manifest!.header.magic).toBe(GDPC_MAGIC);
    expect(manifest!.header.engineMajor).toBe(3);
    expect(manifest!.header.engineMinor).toBe(5);
    expect(manifest!.header.enginePatch).toBe(2);
    expect(manifest!.files).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(manifest!.files[0].path).toBe("res://icon.png");
    expect(manifest!.files[1].path).toBe("res://scripts/player.gd");
  });

  it("should inspect PCK and categorize assets correctly", async () => {
    const adapter = new GodotAdapter();
    const canHandle = await adapter.canHandle(samplePckPath);
    expect(canHandle).toBe(true);

    const manifest = await adapter.inspect(samplePckPath);
    expect(manifest.engine).toBe("godot");
    expect(manifest.engineVersion).toBe("3.5.2");
    expect(manifest.totalFiles).toBe(2);
    expect(manifest.byCategory.texture.count).toBe(1);
    expect(manifest.byCategory.script.count).toBe(1);
  });

  it("should extract PCK files with proper contents", async () => {
    const adapter = new GodotAdapter();
    const outDir = path.join(tmpDir, "extracted");
    const result = await adapter.extract(samplePckPath, { outputDir: outDir });

    expect(result.success).toBe(true);
    expect(result.extractedCount).toBe(2);

    const extractedIcon = await fs.readFile(path.join(outDir, "icon.png"), "utf-8");
    expect(extractedIcon).toBe("PNG_FAKE_IMAGE_DATA_12345");

    const extractedScript = await fs.readFile(path.join(outDir, "scripts", "player.gd"), "utf-8");
    expect(extractedScript).toContain("extends KinematicBody2D");
  });
});
```

`tests/pe-adapter.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach } from "vitest";
import * as fs from "node:fs/promises";
import * as path from "node:path";
import * as os from "node:os";
import { PeResourceParser } from "../src/parsers/pe/pe-resource-parser.js";
import { PeSoftwareAdapter } from "../src/adapters/pe-adapter.js";
import { AdapterRegistry } from "../src/adapters/index.js";

describe("PeResourceParser & PeSoftwareAdapter", () => {
  let tmpDir: string;
  let mockExePath: string;

  beforeEach(async () => {
    tmpDir = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "pe-test-"));
    mockExePath = path.join(tmpDir, "test_app.exe");

    // Construct a minimal valid PE32+ (x64) executable with a .rsrc section
    // DOS Header: 64 bytes
    const dos = Buffer.alloc(128);
    dos.writeUInt16LE(0x5a4d, 0); // "MZ"
    dos.writeInt32LE(128, 0x3c); // e_lfanew = 128

    // PE Header at offset 128
    // PE Signature (4 bytes) = 0x00004550
    // COFF Header (20 bytes)
    // Optional Header (240 bytes for PE32+)
    const pe = Buffer.alloc(4 + 20 + 240);
    pe.writeUInt32LE(0x00004550, 0); // "PE\0\0"
    pe.writeUInt16LE(0x8664, 4); // Machine = AMD64 (x64)
    pe.writeUInt16LE(1, 6); // NumberOfSections = 1
    pe.writeUInt16LE(240, 20); // SizeOfOptionalHeader = 240

    // Optional Header
    pe.writeUInt16LE(0x020b, 24); // Magic = PE32+
    // Data directories start at offset 24 + 112 = 136 in pe buffer
    // Directory index 2 = Resource Table: RVA = 0x2000, Size = 128
    pe.writeUInt32LE(0x2000, 136 + 2 * 8);
    pe.writeUInt32LE(128, 136 + 2 * 8 + 4);

    // Section Header 1 (40 bytes)
    const sec = Buffer.alloc(40);
    sec.write(".rsrc\0\0\0", 0, "ascii");
    sec.writeUInt32LE(0x1000, 8); // VirtualSize
    sec.writeUInt32LE(0x2000, 12); // VirtualAddress (RVA)
    sec.writeUInt32LE(512, 16); // SizeOfRawData
    const fileOffsetRsrc = 128 + pe.length + sec.length; // PointerToRawData
    sec.writeUInt32LE(fileOffsetRsrc, 20);

    // Resource section payload (512 bytes)
    const rsrcPayload = Buffer.alloc(512);
    // Level 1: 1 Type: RT_MANIFEST (24)
    rsrcPayload.writeUInt16LE(0, 12); // numNamed
    rsrcPayload.writeUInt16LE(1, 14); // numId = 1
    rsrcPayload.writeUInt32LE(24, 16); // Type 24 (RT_MANIFEST)
    rsrcPayload.writeUInt32LE(0x80000000 + 32, 20); // Subdir at offset 32

    // Level 2 (offset 32): 1 Name: ID 1
    rsrcPayload.writeUInt16LE(0, 32 + 12);
    rsrcPayload.writeUInt16LE(1, 32 + 14);
    rsrcPayload.writeUInt32LE(1, 48); // Name ID = 1
    rsrcPayload.writeUInt32LE(0x80000000 + 64, 52); // Subdir at offset 64

    // Level 3 (offset 64): 1 Language: ID 0
    rsrcPayload.writeUInt16LE(0, 64 + 12);
    rsrcPayload.writeUInt16LE(1, 64 + 14);
    rsrcPayload.writeUInt32LE(0, 80); // Lang ID 0
    rsrcPayload.writeUInt32LE(96, 84); // Data entry at offset 96 (leaf)

    // Data entry leaf (offset 96): 16 bytes
    // Data RVA = 0x2000 + 200 = 0x20C8
    rsrcPayload.writeUInt32LE(0x20c8, 96);
    const manifestContent = Buffer.from("<assembly/>", "utf-8");
    rsrcPayload.writeUInt32LE(manifestContent.length, 100);

    // Write manifest content at offset 200 in rsrcPayload
    manifestContent.copy(rsrcPayload, 200);

    const fullBinary = Buffer.concat([dos, pe, sec, rsrcPayload]);
    await fs.writeFile(mockExePath, fullBinary);
  });

  afterEach(async () => {
    await fs.rm(tmpDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  });

  it("should parse PE resources and architecture correctly", async () => {
    const info = await PeResourceParser.parse(mockExePath);
    expect(info).not.toBeNull();
    expect(info!.arch).toBe("x64");
    expect(info!.resources).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(info!.resources[0].typeName).toBe("RT_MANIFEST");
    expect(info!.resources[0].size).toBe(Buffer.from("<assembly/>").length);
  });

  it("should inspect PE software executable via PeSoftwareAdapter", async () => {
    const adapter = new PeSoftwareAdapter();
    const canHandle = await adapter.canHandle(mockExePath);
    expect(canHandle).toBe(true);

    const manifest = await adapter.inspect(mockExePath);
    expect(manifest.engine).toBe("pe-software");
    expect(manifest.totalFiles).toBe(1);
    expect(manifest.byCategory.config.count).toBe(1);
  });

  it("should extract resources from PE software application", async () => {
    const adapter = new PeSoftwareAdapter();
    const outDir = path.join(tmpDir, "extracted");
    const result = await adapter.extract(mockExePath, { outputDir: outDir });

    expect(result.success).toBe(true);
    expect(result.extractedCount).toBe(1);

    const manifestFile = path.join(outDir, "manifest", "manifest_1.xml");
    const content = await fs.readFile(manifestFile, "utf-8");
    expect(content).toBe("<assembly/>");
  });

  it("should resolve PeSoftwareAdapter from AdapterRegistry", async () => {
    const { adapter, engine } = await AdapterRegistry.resolveAdapter(mockExePath);
    expect(engine).toBe("pe-software");
    expect(adapter.engine).toBe("pe-software");
  });
});
```

`tests/rea-scanner.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, it, expect, beforeEach, afterEach } from "vitest";
import * as fs from "node:fs/promises";
import * as path from "node:path";
import * as os from "node:os";
import { BinaryScanner } from "../src/rea/binary-scanner.js";
import { ReaBridge } from "../src/rea/rea-bridge.js";

describe("BinaryScanner & ReaBridge", () => {
  let tmpDir: string;
  let mockBinPath: string;

  beforeEach(async () => {
    tmpDir = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), "rea-test-"));
    mockBinPath = path.join(tmpDir, "Game-Win64-Shipping.exe");

    // Create a mock binary buffer containing:
    // 1. MZ / PE header stub
    // 2. High-entropy 64-char hex AES key string: "0x4A6B8D1F3C5E7A9B0C2D4E6F8A1B3C5D7E9F0A2B4C6D8E0F2A4B6C8D0E2F4A6B"
    // 3. Unreal signature text
    const buf = Buffer.alloc(8192);
    // Write MZ signature
    buf.writeUInt16LE(0x5a4d, 0);

    const testAesKey = "0x4a6b8d1f3c5e7a9b0c2d4e6f8a1b3c5d7e9f0a2b4c6d8e0f2a4b6c8d0e2f4a6b";
    buf.write(testAesKey, 512, "ascii");
    buf.write("UnrealEngine Game Client", 1024, "ascii");

    await fs.writeFile(mockBinPath, buf);
  });

  afterEach(async () => {
    await fs.rm(tmpDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  });

  it("should calculate buffer entropy correctly", () => {
    const zeroBuf = Buffer.alloc(100, 0);
    expect(BinaryScanner.calculateEntropy(zeroBuf)).toBe(0);

    // Random-like buffer
    const randomBuf = Buffer.from(Array.from({ length: 256 }, (_, i) => i));
    const entropy = BinaryScanner.calculateEntropy(randomBuf);
    expect(entropy).toBeCloseTo(8, 0.1);
  });

  it("should locate candidate hex AES keys in binary buffer", async () => {
    const scan = await BinaryScanner.scanBinary(mockBinPath);
    expect(scan.candidateAesKeys.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(scan.candidateAesKeys[0].keyHex).toBe(
      "0x4a6b8d1f3c5e7a9b0c2d4e6f8a1b3c5d7e9f0a2b4c6d8e0f2a4b6c8d0e2f4a6b"
    );
    expect(scan.stringsFound).toContain("UnrealEngine signature");
  });

  it("should generate structured REA evidence bundle", async () => {
    const { scan, bundle } = await ReaBridge.inspectBinary(mockBinPath);
    expect(bundle.targetBinary).toBe(mockBinPath);
    expect(bundle.findings.candidateAesKeys.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(bundle.reaTaskPrompt).toContain("Candidate AES keys detected");
  });
});
```
